Se elimina el flag opt-in `requireExtractedAnswerInQuote` y su implementación paralela. No se modifica API, HTTP schema, consultas SQL, ingestion, permisos, E5, ranking, ContextPacket ni writer.

**Seguridad de la inferencia:** `LITERAL` significa solo que el lector ofreció un valor cuya grafía puede vincularse unívocamente a la cita original; NO que el rol sujeto/predicado/dirección/condición del valor sea correcto. `POLARITY` (yes/no/sí) no requiere un token literal en la frase, pero su negación/titular sigue sin prueba independiente. El lector y el guard no crean equivalencias `tres = 3`. Con el guard actual que exige dígitos para preguntas cuantitativas, algunas respuestas positivas siguen retenidas: corregir el **transporte** no resuelve automáticamente el **entailment** ni constituye calibración para relajar el guard. Un `sourceValueSpan` falso, extralimitado, en fuente oculta, repetido o con hash/original distinto debe fallar cerrado, sin exponer campos `answerEvidence` en trazas HTTP.

**Pruebas en control:** caso fuente con Unicode astral antes de `tres`; propagación reader→verificación→LAYERED y guard que continúa reteniendo cardinal escrito; `Faro-6` no permite extraer una `6` como cantidad; `TracerProvider` no es `Tracer`; `three` no se normaliza a `3`; Unicode NFC + variantes de espacios son exactas en los offsets originales; duplicados, falsificación de offsets, cita adulterada, respuestas tipo negación y pruebas de fallo en `CONTRADICTS/INSUFFICIENT`. La fuente de pruebas es inventada para el contrato y no mide precisión lingüística del proveedor. Los modelos reales y HTTP de §§42–47 siguen sin un nuevo holdout; no reutilizar sus tasas como aceptación de este corte.

**Gobernanza y deuda aún abierta:** el siguiente paso de implementación no debe añadir otro adaptador paralelo ni restaurar el flag. Deberá diseñar y aceptar el único evaluador de **relación completa** (sujeto/predicado/polaridad/cantidad/tiempo/condición) que consuma el valor fuente y emita pruebas comparables, con evaluación prospectiva de falsos respaldos y falsas abstenciones; luego medir pasajes, packet y writer a través de Fastify real. El clasificador documental del ADR 0005 no aumenta autoridad de SOURCE y está fuera de este corte. Quedan pendientes mejoras de _answer-bearing excerpt recall_, disponibilidad real del proveedor, traducción multilingüe de consultas sin contaminar la pregunta de adjudicación y la cobertura por afirmación del writer.

**Gate inalterado hasta esa aceptación:** S2 OPEN · E5 SHADOW · S3–S5 BLOCKED · PR #38 DRAFT/UNMERGED.

**Hallazgos adicionales de cableado que el transporte de valor NO cierra:**

- En `packages/retrieval/src/layered-answerability.ts`, `assessRetrievalAnswerabilityWithLayeredAdmission` crea los inputs reales con `window.map(hit => ({query, hit}))`; NO pasa `queryProposition` ni `candidateProposition`. Por tanto `ExactStructuredPropositionMatcher`, aunque implementado y probado, **no interviene en el HTTP LAYERED actual**. La admisión positiva depende de la propuesta del lector, del vínculo literal y de controles deterministas; el nuevo campo `requestedFact` sigue siendo propuesto por el modelo, no una certificación independiente de la relación. La próxima evolución debe conectar proyecciones verificadas desde el SOURCE gobernado y una representación consistente de pregunta, con condiciones, dirección, negación, ámbito temporal y cuantificadores, bajo un único contrato. No interpolar proposiciones inventadas en tiempo de respuesta.
- En `apps/api/src/runtime-config.ts`, el tiempo de una solicitud al proveedor lector está configurado por defecto en **30.000 ms**, mientras que el presupuesto de admisión por lote es **25.000 ms** y el timeout HTTP Fastify en `server.ts` es **30.000 ms**. `ReaderEvidenceVerifier` hace shortlist de cuatro y concurrencia por defecto dos, con posibles rondas adaptativas. `QueryConditionedSemanticEvidenceReader.readBatch` impone un timeout sobre el **lote completo**; puede perder resultados de candidatos ya terminados si alguna llamada excede ese presupuesto. Esta es una incompatibilidad operativa configurada y comprobada en el código, no prueba de fallo universal de todos los proveedores ni un motivo para ampliar el timeout sin medir GPU/colas. Registrar proveedor, espera, llamada y decisión por candidato; diseñar deadline/cancelación cooperativos y preservación fail-closed de decisiones ya completadas, conservando request budgets y evitando inferencias tardías que sobrecarguen GPU.
- El validador `quantitativeEvidenceMatches` sigue detectando números en `evidence.valueText` como condición necesaria; no prueba una relación. No convertir `tres` a `3` ni admitir por simple coincidencia de número hasta que el evaluador de relación completa y sus controles independientes estén calibrados. La **reparación de transporte** de esta sección no se describe como corrección demostrada del recall EN/ES.

**Métricas de estado documentadas para el corte:** 69/69 pruebas focalizadas (incluye seis nuevas del contrato); suite de recuperación completa 640/640; matriz del monorepo 87/87 (47 reusadas de caché); docs, higiene (1.069 entradas), contratos, secretos, límites de módulos, generalidad, holdout, Prettier y diff limpios en local. Estas validaciones son de implementación y regresión, no evaluación E2E de calidad con lector real. La CI de GitHub sobre el futuro SHA debe comprobarse por separado. Falta aceptación LAYERED/ContextPacket/writer con fuentes nuevas y etiquetado independiente.

## 50. S2 — Endurecimiento del guard con valor fuente tipado (26 octubre; no aceptación)

**Problema residual confirmado después del commit `6edada05`:** el contrato de `answerEvidence` atravesaba reader, validación de procedencia, admisión y guard, pero `hardStructuralFactsSatisfied` seguía comprobando números y años presentes en `evidence.valueText`, sin preguntar si **ese mismo número** era el valor extraído. Una fuente auténtica sobre aprobaciones y registros de archivo podía permitir que un número incidental pasara la condición estructural. Este punto se suma, sin sustituirlos, a los fallos de selección de pasaje, lectura de roles, traducción y disponibilidad de proveedor documentados en §§42–49.

**Cambio sobre la arquitectura productiva única:** `packages/retrieval/src/evidence-admission.ts` reusa el `QueryConditionedAnswerEvidence` ya validado por `validateSourceBoundVerification`. Para consultas con requisito `QUANTITY` o `DATE_YEAR`, y cuando existe un slot del reader, exige que `kind=LITERAL` y la **porción exacta de fuente de `valueSpan`**, no cualquier número cercano, satisfaga también la condición estructural preexistente. Si la cita cumple pero el valor no, retiene con `STRUCTURAL_ANSWER_VALUE_REQUIRED_FACT_MISSING`; si ni siquiera la cita cumple, conserva `STRUCTURAL_REQUIRED_FACT_MISSING`. Una respuesta `POLARITY` no es cantidad ni fecha. No se crea un nuevo verificador, una nueva vía de búsqueda, un campo HTTP ni un adaptador legacy; se refuerza el guard propietario del criterio. No se cambia E5, ranking, ACL, ContextPacket o Writer.

**Límite fundamental aún abierto:** tener un dígito literal único dentro del pasaje **no comprueba que represente la cantidad del sujeto preguntado**. Un modelo que atribuya `17` del archivo al número de aprobaciones podría continuar proponiendo `SUPPORTS` si no hay verificación independiente de la relación completa; este endurecimiento solo impide que un valor textual distinto herede accidentalmente el dígito cercano. `tres` versus `3` sigue sin equivalencia probada; la retención conservadora es visible y se mantiene. No vender esta mejora como mayor recall ni dar S2 por aceptado.

**Regresión de producto:** casos nuevos en el test de transferencia del reader obligan a rechazar el valor `approval` aunque una cita contenga `17 archive files`, y una fecha incidental aunque la respuesta extraída sea `approval`. Un `tres` auténtico continúa retenido por el guard previo; el caso con `3` fuente auténtico continúa estructuralmente admisible, sin certificación de entailment. Los tests usan un reader simulado y no son una cohorte de evaluación independiente. Prioridad siguiente: contrato de relación atomizada con sujeto/predicado/dirección/polaridad/condiciones/tiempo y negativa adversarial, pruebas HTTP Fastify con nuevas fuentes EN/ES, trazabilidad ContextPacket → claims de writer y herramientas con identidad real, junto a p95 y falsos respaldos/abstenciones. **S2 OPEN · E5 SHADOW · S3–S5 BLOCKED · PR #38 DRAFT/UNMERGED.**

## 51. S2 — Resultado rechazado del corte de roles y defensa de procedencia (26 octubre; no aceptación)

**Experimento independiente:** la propuesta `d6dc7bfe6c0d602d883780f086c09f6acc78b402` endureció la comparación de roles explícitos y cambió la atribución de abstenciones `SEMANTIC_READER → STRUCTURED_PROPOSITION`. El benchmark previamente congelado `structured-proposition-family-disjoint-v1` registró desarrollo **6/8 = 0,75** y heldout **4/8 = 0,50** (familias EN/ES disjuntas). Fallaron únicamente expectativas predeclaradas sobre la _capa_ de abstención (sujeto, predicado y campo requerido vacío), no casos que pasaran falsamente a `ANSWERS`. CI remoto y release-assurance en el SHA `d6dc7bfe` fallaron; otros 12 workflows finalizaron verdes y 9 quedaron omitidos. **Rechazado/no promovido:** no se cambió el manifiesto congelado ni sus resultados esperados después de observarlos. Se restauró en un commit posterior el comportamiento anterior del matcher general y su pipeline; el historial del ensayo rechazado permanece en Git. Para probar un contrato nuevo de atribución se necesitará un protocolo y cohortes versionados y predeclarados, no editar el holdout antiguo hasta que salga verde.

**Mejora independiente en el límite de SOURCE gobernado:** `PostgresTemporalTruthStore.governedFactEvidenceForDocument` exige que el documento consultado pertenezca al mismo space/vault, esté ACTIVE y coincida exactamente en ruta y `current_revision` con el `SearchHit`, además de comprobar el path frente a la credencial efectiva ANTES de buscar hechos o evidencia. La vía `governed-proposition-shadow.ts` exige polaridad explícita y se abstiene ante condiciones, ámbito temporal, unidad o modalidad de la pregunta aún no implementados por el matcher base, en vez de omitir silenciosamente esos roles. Ninguna afirmación de estos resultados cambia la búsqueda, el writer o la admisión HTTP: es **shadow con query-proposition explícita**; falta el constructor legítimo de pregunta y el join de SOURCE real con ACL/revision/claims para el flujo productivo.

**Evidencia requerida:** PostgreSQL desechable real, migraciones, la prueba `governed-proposition-shadow.integration.test.ts` con hit autorizado y negativos de path/revisión/qualifier/polaridad; clean checkout CI same-SHA; posterior heldout _nuevo e independiente_ para roles completos, EN/ES, y E2E authenticated SOURCE→KNOWLEDGE→retrieval→ContextPacket→writer. No confundir prueba de unión documental con entailment. S2 OPEN · E5 SHADOW · S3–S5 BLOCKED · PR #38 DRAFT/UNMERGED.

## 52. S2 — SOURCE attestation for governed proposition shadow (2026-10-26; separate from semantic holdout)

**Ownership / invariant.** The governed-temporal source bridge previously matched authorized fact/evidence IDs and current KNOWLEDGE document path/revision but accepted **any** `document_evidence.relation_type`, even a conflicting edge, and did not require a current source or an artifact matching the source's present `sha256`. A matching excerpt is insufficient evidence authority. No schema/API change was required: `PostgresTemporalTruthStore.governedFactEvidenceForDocument` now selects evidence exclusively through `document_evidence.relation_type='supported_by'`, a source with the same space/vault and `status='ACTIVE'`, and (when an artifact is referenced) an artifact belonging to that source whose `source_hash` equals the current source `sha256`. Its previous exact KNOWLEDGE document/current-revision/path and fact support checks remain mandatory. SOURCE and reviewed KNOWLEDGE identities remain separate.

**Adversarial execution.** Ran a _fresh_, disposable `pgvector/pgvector:pg16` PostgreSQL `akp_s2_test`, applied migrations and rebuilt `@akp/postgres`. `apps/api/test/governed-proposition-shadow.integration.test.ts` PASS covers the previously authorized success plus replacement of the supporting edge with a conflicting relation, withdrawal of the underlying source, mismatched artifact source hash, and subsequent restoration; all three negative mutations produce zero resolved fact bundles, and the valid restored chain again produces one `ANSWERS`. Existing stale document revision/path, actor path scope, duplicate-quote and unsupported query qualification negatives remain covered. Container stopped/removed; no operator data was modified. **No independent EN/ES semantic-role accuracy or authenticated HTTP→ContextPacket→Writer evidence is claimed.**

**Open role/query obligation.** The query proposition remains explicit caller input, not independently derived from natural language. Therefore an `ANSWERS` decision in this shadow measures exact matching against an asserted query structure, not full natural-language entailment. Do not expose this caller projection as authenticated HTTP admission, accept an LLM's query roles as truth, bypass source ACL, or report this provenance-only change as the S2 acceptance. The frozen family-disjoint matcher benchmark remains untouched after §51 rollback. Gate: S2 OPEN / E5 SHADOW / S3–S5 BLOCKED.

## 53. S2 — Exact excerpt digest + SOURCE locator revision binding (2026-10-26; integrity precondition only)

**Observed gap after §52:** `governedFactEvidenceForDocument` required an ACTIVE SOURCE and matching artifact `source_hash`, but a persisted evidence locator could identify an _unrelated_ source revision, and a later change to `evidence.excerpt` could leave `content_hash` unchanged while `projectSupportedTemporalFact` still accepted the excerpt and proposed a role match. The existing Worker `selectEvidenceFragment/assertEvidenceFragmentIntegrity` emits SHA-256 of the exact UTF-8 excerpt and carries `locator.source_hash` from the extracted SOURCE artifact. This cut reuses that contract, without adding synonyms, query heuristics or another general admission engine.

**Single source/projection boundary:** PostgreSQL requires `e.locator->>'source_hash'=s.sha256` at the governed-evidence join; missing/foreign/old locator revisions now fail closed. `packages/retrieval/src/proposition-projection.ts` verifies `SHA256(UTF8(evidence.excerpt)) === evidence.contentHash` for every evidence ID in the support set, in addition to same space/vault, source identity and other existing governed checks. The digest is calculated from _exact bytes_ of the persisted excerpt; even a Unicode canonical composition change is rejected (no normalization or text substitution). A missing or mismatched hash makes the projection unavailable, before a semantic-role verdict can be attributed to SOURCE.

**Measured evidence:** Local selected Retrieval unit tests for proposition projection/evidence admission: **23 passed**. New adversarial cases include a corrupted hash, changed excerpt, Unicode normalization difference and altered hash case. Fresh disposable `pgvector/pgvector:pg16`, database `akp_s2_test`, migrations and rebuilt Retrieval/Postgres, then the real governed shadow integration: **PASS** with a negative mismatched source revision in the evidence locator, corrupt persisted excerpt hash, tampered stored excerpt, and restoration after each mutation; existing source withdrawal, conflicting document edge, stale artifact, path, revision and qualifier tests remain present. Container stopped/removed. General monorepo/CI gates recorded separately in the PR comment if completed.

**Security/meaning limits:** This does **not** re-fetch raw SOURCE bytes from immutable object storage, prove excerpt containment in the canonical DocumentArtifact or verify the semantic relation of natural-language question → proposed subject/predicate/object/polarity/condition. Hashing a stored excerpt detects tampering relative to its stored digest, but simultaneous malicious replacement of both excerpt and digest would need an independently re-read raw artifact to detect. The SOURCE locator version is an asserted persisted binding, not itself an externally certified content proof. Continue with independently authorized raw bytes + exact structural locator rebind, then prospective family-disjoint EN/ES role/citation evaluation; no HTTP LAYERED promotion. S2 OPEN · E5 SHADOW · S3–S5 BLOCKED.

## 54. S2 — Immutable raw-text SOURCE re-read in governed shadow (2026-10-26)

**Prior gate:** The last published §53 checkpoint `57d8bbf7` completed same-SHA GitHub CI with 14 successful / 9 skipped / 0 failed runs, before this change. §53's locator and excerpt SHA checks still trusted the persisted evidence excerpt as the root of byte content; replacing the excerpt _and_ digest together could manufacture apparent internal integrity.

**Single bounded authority.** The existing governed-temporal proposition shadow, not production HTTP admission, now requires an injected `ObjectStore`. The PostgreSQL fact/evidence join returns the raw SOURCE `sha256`, `object_key`, `byte_size` and media type from its already-authorized active source row; these are **not** taken from a SearchHit or user parameter. A new shadow verifier reuses `readAndVerifyRawEvidence` from the existing audited raw-export reader, which independently fetches raw bytes, caps transfer at **2 MiB**, verifies exact object key, byte length and whole-object SHA-256, then decodes strict UTF-8 and requires an **exact, unique occurrence** of each complete persisted excerpt. Any missing store/object, read error, invalid UTF-8, changed bytes, ambiguous repeated quote or size/hash/key mismatch abstains before structured proposition matching. The only accepted locator currently is the Worker's explicit `kind=source`, `path=source:<sourceId>`, `source_hash=SHA256` fallback for text/plain or text/markdown variants. Structural paragraph/table locators and all PDF/OCR/binary artifacts **do not pass** this proof and remain unadmitted; text searching inside PDF bytes is not a sound extractor attestation. All evidence IDs in a governed support set must independently pass.

**Evidence and scope:** Focused unit tests exercise valid immutable text, stale/altered bytes, repeated passages, malformed UTF-8, misleading locator/key/size/media type, missing and inaccessible store; existing hash/ACL/stale-revision/semantic-shadow guards remain intact. A newly migrated disposable PostgreSQL integration invokes the real shadow with an in-memory `ObjectStore` adapter returning controlled raw bytes, including byte mutations and refusing unsupported media types. This is **real PostgreSQL + simulated object transport**, **not** an authenticated MinIO deployment test, and is not yet a SOURCE extraction/compilation→Writer E2E. Test/check results recorded separately. No change to default LAYERED/search flags, review permissions, frozen holdout manifest or `develop`.

**Deliberate limits:** A raw literal is not the same as a proven structural page/paragraph/table/row locator, especially for OCR or binary sources. The raw-to-DocumentArtifact extractor transformation, current permission snapshot consistency across async reads, query role/condition verification, ContextPacket/Writer per-claim grounded citations, disjoint EN/ES independent acceptance, and live provider timing/timeout evidence remain OPEN. No governed query proposition can be accepted as user-query truth merely because source bytes were verified. **S2 OPEN / E5 SHADOW / S3–S5 BLOCKED.**

## 55. S2 — CI semantic gap diagnosis and completion-safe LAYERED reader batch (2026-10-26)

**Priority re-established:** PR #38 concerns _semantic sufficiency of the answer_, not provenance hardening alone. The previous §51–§54 source attestations are prerequisites and must not be counted as better answer precision. The latest same-SHA `semantic-retrieval-40364320450` CI artifact (`e21c4e63`, 14 successful / 9 skipped workflow runs overall) was read and compared to the product path before engineering this change. Its **deterministic** generic family-disjoint generalization set reports, for 128 heldout questions (100 answerable, 28 unanswerable), positive answerable recall **0.26**, no-answer false-acceptance **6/28 = 0.2142857**, and admitted-unit precision **0.5306122**. Across 261 synthetic questions, negative-role adversaries include WRONG_SUBJECT (**6/19 false accepted**), TABLE_ROW (**3/12**), SAME_VOCABULARY (**7/31**), and MISSING_SLOT (**5/25**). The same deterministic benchmark has 0 answerable recall in CROSS_LINGUAL (48 answerable) and NEGATIVE_ANSWER (50), and the YES_NO intent has 0 answerable recall (67 answerable). These challenge strata overlap and must not be added as independent sample sizes. **This is deterministic supplied-candidate admission, not LAYERED with a live reader or final answer scoring; upstream retrieval is explicitly unmeasured in this report.** Do not imply a measured model improvement from this data.

The same committed CI artifact's real PostgreSQL/pinned E5 synthetic SOURCE-span comparison reports E5 hybrid Recall@1 of **24/24** gold units versus exact+lexical **0/24**, but _only_ 8/24 positive gold candidates have deterministic support and 4/24 unanswerable questions have a candidate with apparent deterministic support. Its `admissionMeasured` and `generatedAnswerCorrectnessMeasured` are false; this is a ranking-versus-admission diagnostic only. NLI/E5-relation shadow benchmarks explicitly have no calibrated promotable candidate. The original-query relation check and authentic EN/ES final-answer correctness therefore remain primary open acceptance criteria.

**Actual production loss isolated:** `apps/api/src/routes/search.ts` sends the original 1200-character atomic `SearchHit.excerpt` through LAYERED answerability; broader `parentContext` goes only to the reasoning/writer path and is not itself exact source-bound admission evidence. Matching the document or even a leaf unit does not prove that the reader saw the answer-bearing sentence. Parent windows may only be promoted after same-revision source/locator authentication, non-truncating quote preservation and paired false-admission testing, not by copying formatted `parentContext` into the excerpt.

**This patch's behavioral repair:** Under the prior `QueryConditionedSemanticEvidenceReader.readBatch` timeout, a single slow reader caused _every_ candidate to return `INSUFFICIENT`, even if other candidate calls had completed with valid exact source spans. The same owner `QueryConditionedEvidenceBatchOptions` now has a narrow, in-memory `onCandidateCompleted` callback. `ReaderEvidenceVerifier.verifyBatch` reports individual completions with no extra model calls, and the adapter validates each early result using the existing `verificationVerdict`/source-bound contract. On **batch timeout only**, already completed, source-validated rows are preserved; unfinished rows still emit `SEMANTIC_READER_TIMEOUT` as `INSUFFICIENT`. Malformed spans remain insufficient, non-timeout batch errors do not salvage prior callbacks, late callback results cannot promote an answer after the deadline, and the product `StructuralEvidenceGuard` still adjudicates the retained successes. Successful normal batches retain their previous semantics. No changes to provider timeouts, model, shortlist, thresholds, feature flags, ACL, source rebind, user data, or frozen oracle. A regression in a sparse output array was caught and fixed before final test validation.

**Proof boundary:** Dedicated deterministic tests of one completed source-bound candidate alongside a stalled candidate, malicious span, non-timeout failure, and two actually concurrent `ReaderEvidenceVerifier` calls pass alongside existing reader/admission tests (67 focused tests). This proves _partial-timeout transport continuity_; it does **not** prove semantic entailment, model p95, false-admission reduction, English/Spanish end-to-end gains, new answer-bearing excerpt recall or S2 acceptance. Real LAYERED model evaluation with independent question-role labels remains mandatory; this local device had no reachable Ollama service and limited free memory, so no model quality score is invented. The product problem remains S2 OPEN · E5 SHADOW · S3–S5 BLOCKED; PR #38 DRAFT/UNMERGED.

## 56. S2 — source-bound cardinal words reach LAYERED without incidental numeral promotion (2026-10-26)

**Observed root cause on real local Qwen3.5, replayed before editing policy.** Previous locally captured `OpenAICompatibleEvidenceReader` v5 (`qwen3.5:9b-q4_K_M`, immutable Ollama digest `56671c2a…179a3c90`, `reasoningEffort:"none"`, strict JSON schema, `maxOutputTokens=256`) judged four known Atlas-9/Faro-6 EN/ES DEV source/question cases, with SHA-matched original source excerpts and no development gold in prompts. It emitted two source-bound literal `SUPPORTS` and two `INSUFFICIENT`. Actual, separate replay through the product `StructuralEvidenceGuard` showed one positive EN `ANSWERS` and one positive ES `INSUFFICIENT / STRUCTURAL_REQUIRED_FACT_MISSING`; the latter had the source-bound `answerText="tres"`, exact UTF-16 value span and a quote saying **tres security reviewers**. The existing `quantitativeEvidenceMatches` required ASCII numeric digits in the answer text. This is an _independently reproduced downstream false abstention after successful reader extraction_, not an upstream retrieval or model inference failure.

**Single contract repair, no parallel reader:** `exactSourceCardinalWord` accepts a deliberately finite set of _whole-token small cardinal words, 0–20, in EN/ES_. At the existing final `hardStructuralFactsSatisfied` guard only, a word can satisfy the `QUANTITY` cue **solely** when the v5 reader supplied `answerEvidence.kind="LITERAL"`, the answer text is that entire cardinal token, and the current `valueSpan` has already passed the existing unique exact-source UTF-16 rebind **inside the same selected quote**. The monthly/time-unit scope requirement is factored into `quantityMeasureScopeMatches` and applies equally to digit and word values. No change to `quantitativeEvidenceMatches`'s digit-only requirement for untyped/legacy windows, and no conversion of extracted `three` into a claimed `3` offset. Numeric fragments in hyphenated IDs such as Faro-6, headings, ordinal list markers, dates, unsupported multiword amounts and duplicate/unbound value mentions do not gain authority. No model text, value, quote or private sources enter API traces or public payloads.

**Measured paired no-inference replay** over the same four frozen Qwen3.5 v5 judgments:

- Previous guard: 1/2 source-bound positive author-labeled DEV answers eligible, 0/2 DEV negative admissions.
- New guard: **2/2** eligible, **0/2** negative admissions. The new success is exactly the Faro-6 `tres` value; both models' provider responses, quote bytes and source roles are unchanged. This is a four-case _known developer-labelled test_ of **post-reader false-abstention removal**, NOT an independent semantic precision or full HTTP end-to-end uplift.
- Source-bound count regression tests exercise missing answer slot, wrong offset, repeated cardinal value, attempted ID-suffix count, monthly scope without evidence, wrong-year and ordinal gating, existing numeric values, and the real in-memory `ReaderEvidenceVerifier`→`QueryConditionedSemanticEvidenceReader`→`LayeredEvidenceAdmissionPipeline`. The two legacy tests explicitly asserting the old _digit-only veto for a source-bound word_ were updated to specify the intentional new contract; **no frozen holdout/benchmark oracle was changed**.

**Still unproven:** the model's judgment of subject, predicate, direction, conditions, negation or causality remains fallible even if `tres` is authentically present inside the requested quote. A name `Faro-6` cannot establish a count, but even a valid exact count under the wrong actor/relation is insufficient. Additional independent question→claim model-role adjudication, genuinely new source-family-disjoint bilingual labels, false-source-admission upper bounds, source-window acquisition for truncated excerpts, actual signed/original SOURCE→reviewed KNOWLEDGE→authenticated HTTP→Writer citation atomization, and cold/warm p95 are not discharged here. Do not enable LAYERED by default or merge PR #38 on this narrow real-world failure repair. **S2 OPEN, E5 SHADOW, S3–S5 BLOCKED**.

## 57. S2 — 261-case source-supplied Qwen2.5 live reader, typed-value attrition and bounded retry (2026-10-26)

**Recovered canonical corpus, not invented replacement:** `evals/generic/evidence-admission` contains **261** historical EN/ES questions (development 133, heldout 128) from eight fixed domains, **204 answerable** and **57 unanswerable**. Both splits and gold annotations were public/inspected in previous PR experiments; they are **NOT an untouched independent S2 acceptance holdout**. The originally designed full multi-candidate deterministic admission reran unchanged on HEAD `8f810cb1`: dev positive recall **19.2%**, negative FAR **20.7%**, admitted-unit precision **56.8%**; heldout **26.0%**, **21.4%**, **53.1%**. Aggregate report stayed in ignored `.work/s2-dev-261-deterministic-20261026.json` (report SHA-256 `f0b64a2b7895bd222700c7a839882a9e3763036a428af61b067ac7b3aa40a654`).

**Actual LLM, 261/261 completed:** separate source-supplied reader-v5 run used installed `qwen2.5:7b-instruct`, Ollama OpenAI-compatible JSON schema, `reasoningEffort:none`, 256 output tokens, `ReaderEvidenceVerifier` and the real `StructuralEvidenceGuard`. For **positive** questions, the harness supplied the first known answer-bearing unit, and for **negative** questions selected the maximum lexical-overlap distractor (stable tie-break). Each question was sent with **one source** and no gold labels; this is deliberately **not retrieval ranking, whole HTTP/ContextPacket/Writer, or production E2E**. A SHA-checked, crash-resumable runner guarded RAM and unloaded Ollama between batches, without changing any existing model or working user process. Raw source-bound capture (not in Git) is `.work/s2-261-qwen25-v5-20261026/RAW_UNSCORED.json`, SHA-256 `f9eabcb1610f9d9358cb0ed71904fcfed64ec830524931d0aacff47358e0d768`. Postrun scoring read gold only _after capture_.

| Same source candidate, historical 261-question task |  Deterministic | Real Qwen2.5 reader v5 + guard |
| --------------------------------------------------- | -------------: | -----------------------------: |
| Positive source admission                           | 46/204 = 22.5% |            **126/204 = 61.8%** |
| Negative source false admission                     |   7/57 = 12.3% |               **1/57 = 1.75%** |
| Admitted-unit label precision                       |  46/53 = 86.8% |            **126/127 = 99.2%** |
| Strict question result                              | 96/261 = 36.8% |            **182/261 = 69.7%** |

These figures use historical developer-authored unit labels, not independently verified claim/quote-level semantics. Qwen raw inference alone is not production HTTP p95; per-candidate call median **2.34 s**, p95 **4.09 s** including cold starts. Across EN/ES challenges, `NEGATIVE_ANSWER` positive 47/50, `QUANTITY` positive 33/40, `YES_NO` positive 63/67; `RATIONALE` 1/19, `DEFINITION` 4/22 and `PROCEDURE` 3/19 remain critical weaknesses. Detailed failure groups: **53** otherwise-positive candidates with a nonuniquely source-bound answer value, **13** incomplete v5 responses, **10** otherwise-positive `READER_FOUND_NO_ANSWER`, and one positive with a quote not locatable. A nonanswerable inverse relation (`sa-q33`) was incorrectly admitted as `no`: the quoted source described **restaurant personnel notifying the pest company**, not the reversed company→restaurant proposition. Correct literal value presence is therefore not full semantic role entailment.

**Rejected simplification:** an offline shadow rule requiring an explicit negator in every negative answer's quote blocks that single observed inverse error **but falsely vetoes 17 of 46 positive `no` answers** in the same dataset. It was not implemented. Rule-direction and conditions must be independently source-grounded, not decided by cue words.

**Source-literal prompt A/B, real Qwen2.5, 60/60 completed:** a shadow-only second prompt required one contiguous literal answer value. Matched outcomes: initial v5 **12/52** positive, shadow **25/52**, **18** positive gains, **5** old positive losses, and **0/8** false admissions in both arms; no model/provider failures. Selection was **post hoc** and deliberately enriched for observed unbound-value failures (30), with admitted controls (12), no-answer controls (12), and remaining rejects (6), so the sample cannot support promotion or statistical safety. In 30 unbound-value positives, **13** became source-bound; globally replacing the prompt would regress five previously accepted items.

**Narrow code experiment, disabled by default:** `EvidenceReaderInput.exactSourceValueOnly` modifies **only** stage 3 of the existing v5 prompt when explicitly requested. The existing `ReaderEvidenceVerifier` accepts `retryUnboundValueOnce`; a single bounded second inference may occur **only** after the original judgment was `ANSWERS`, with a real uniquely located quote, but the typed extracted answer could not bind to a unique source substring. This second judgment must independently pass the existing quote→source offsets→exact literal value→structural guard; no retries on absent answer, quote mismatch, provider error or multiple failed attempts. The prior successful v5 path and default prompt are untouched, so the five observed regressions from _full prompt replacement_ are not introduced by this first-pass-preserving retry logic. Explicit API env `AKP_EVIDENCE_READER_RETRY_UNBOUND_VALUE_ONCE=true` is **OFF by default**, identified in verifier id. It is not evidence of independent semantic verification, and doubling an affected candidate's inference budget has unmeasured tail latency under real authenticated request load. Targeted live 53-case recovery against this actual opt-in implementation is separately planned; the previous 60-pair prompt A/B cannot substitute for that execution or a genuinely blind adversarial cohort.

**Acceptance still OPEN:** the 261 corpus does not demonstrate full LAYERED retrieval ranking, actual selected quote answer-slot/subject/predicate/time correctness, source→KNOWLEDGE authorization and original immutable raw bytes through Writer citation, fresh disjoint EN/ES adjudication, full-request provider p95/token efficiency or operational no-availability. Preserve frozen oracles; retain S2 OPEN, E5 SHADOW, S3–S5 BLOCKED, PR #38 DRAFT/UNMERGED, no default model/flag promotion.

## 58. S2 — completed Qwen2.5 bounded-value retry and no-answer adversarial cohort (2026-10-26)

**Captured live model, not only simulated decisions.** From the original, fully captured 261 historical source-supplied question→passage v5 evaluation (§57), selected the exact **53** positive cases whose first-run reason was `READER_ANSWER_VALUE_NOT_UNIQUELY_SOURCE_BOUND`. Re-ran the same source and question, with installed `qwen2.5:7b-instruct` pinned to its Ollama digest, `reasoningEffort:none`, and the **actual opt-in** `ReaderEvidenceVerifier({ retryUnboundValueOnce:true })` followed by the same final `StructuralEvidenceGuard`. All 53 requests completed. **31/53** ultimately admitted with source-bound values: **30** were admitted following the specifically flagged second inference; **1** was admitted on the newly sampled first inference (model variability, not a retry benefit); **22** remained insufficient. **50/53** triggered exactly one retry, for **103 total model calls**; no provider failures. Completion p50 **7,394 ms**, p95 **18,738 ms**, p99 **19,855 ms** per source candidate; English p95 **9,177 ms** and Spanish p95 **19,121 ms**. These include new model warm/cold load conditions, not actual HTTP/ContextPacket/Writer p95. Pinned local uns cored capture `.work/s2-261-value-retry-live-20261026/RAW_UNSCORED.json` SHA-256 `09da76c8bdddbdc0a13a97bf8241ff0a02991adc0762e3129866f2a74a869133`. Scoring was performed **after** live capture from the developer-authored historical label matrix; no source family is an independent unseen gold set.

**Separate negative control at the same actual opt-in boundary.** Re-ran **all 57** negative/adversarial questions of the same 261 corpus, using their original, SHA-pinned hard-distractor source. All 57 completed, **58 total calls**, **one** input triggered a second pass, **zero** second-pass negative admissions, **one total false admission** produced by the **first pass**, zero provider failures. Negative p50 **1,472 ms**, p95 **5,206 ms**, p99 **10,870 ms**. Raw local negative capture `.work/s2-261-negative-retry-live-20261026/RAW_UNSCORED.json` SHA-256 `fe882c6fdc36a71a98b9d95c373af2ce55795a0c01165e5f0e5642cab5ebb45b`. The persistent false admission is exactly `sa-q33`: the passage says the restaurant's personnel informs the pest-control company, while the question asks whether the pest-control company must inform the restaurant. Qwen returns `no` and passes literal quote and value binding, but **the inverse relation never proves that negative proposition**. The second attempt is not invoked on it. The prior 1/57 false admission rate is unchanged, not zero.

**Answer-type adversarial diagnosis exposes historical-unit-label overstatement.** Among 72 historical source-admitted `POLARITY` outputs (yes/no/true/false), some `no` answers were generated for **nonbinary questions** such as “How often is each engineer on call?” and “¿Cuándo hay que cambiar el aceite de la freidora?”. Such a response is visibly not an answer to the requested value even if the source unit is labeled positive. This means the reported **126/127 (99.2%) label precision** in §57 is _source-unit label precision_, NOT answer/claim-correct precision; the distinction is materially important. Two proposed deterministic **answer-type** gates were tested in separate non-writing shadow replay and **rejected**: reusing the existing cue classifier would veto **9** source-admitted polarity judgments (**8 historically positive, 1 negative `sa-q33`**), while a new EN/ES interrogative-shape grammar would veto **10 positive units** but **not** the inverse negative. The latter missed condition-led valid binary questions such as “If X happens, can Y be released?”. It is unsound to turn such lexical grammatical policy into a general semantic adjudicator. No gate was implemented.

**Decision and remaining hard obstacle:** Leave `AKP_EVIDENCE_READER_RETRY_UNBOUND_VALUE_ONCE=false` by default even though 30 exact-value recoveries are real. The per-candidate 18.7s p95 threatens the current 25s full semantic batch admission budget when multiple candidates are processed. The historical developer-authored 53/57 source-supplied cohorts establish neither a prospective FAR bound nor complete role/condition/polarity truth, fresh source-family-disjoint independent EN/ES annotation, retrieval Recall@k or authorized SOURCE→KNOWLEDGE→HTTP→ContextPacket→Writer citation correctness. Future S2 intervention must bind the **whole query proposition and source proposition** under one authoritative governed source relation and independently adjudicate the direction/negative claim, not rely on a second model agreeing or on lexical cues. The existing optional reader retry is bounded and fail-closed at the source-value level, but is **not accepted for default release**. Keep **S2 OPEN · E5 SHADOW · S3–S5 BLOCKED**; PR #38 stays OPEN/DRAFT.

## 59. S2 — incomplete reader judgments are semantic abstentions, not global provider outages (2026-10-26)

**Additional prospective configuration diagnostic on a known corpus.** Of the 204 historical answer-bearing supplied-source questions in the complete 261-case Qwen2.5 v5 capture (§57), 13 returned `READER_ERROR:EVIDENCE_READER_REPLY_INCOMPLETE_ANSWER`. Increased only that same `qwen2.5:7b-instruct` reader's output budget from **256 to 768** on those **13** known failures plus **13** historical negative source candidates; no gold reached the model. All **26/26** inference calls completed. Outcomes: **1/13 positive source units recovered**, **12/13 remained incomplete structured `ANSWERS` replies** missing a requested-fact, literal answer or source quote field; **0/13** negative admits. Positive p95 **13.322s**, negative p95 **2.644s** per supplied candidate; model remains available in local Ollama and no providers were changed. Archived private ignored capture `.work/s2-261-output768-probe-20261026/RAW_UNSCORED.json`, SHA-256 `3ad73649fba5e0ada0cd041ef13e522467750ad5eb16fbcd68d157f00dcc1b4b`. **Rejected the blanket token-budget increase**: the majority of incomplete structured claims remain incomplete; a bigger budget does not manufacture the missing proof and may worsen latency.

**Architectural failure diagnosed at the adaptive shortlist boundary.** `parseEvidenceReaderJudgment` correctly throws `EVIDENCE_READER_REPLY_INCOMPLETE_ANSWER` when the model says `ANSWERS` but leaves `needed`, `answer` or `answer_span` missing/empty. Yet `ReaderEvidenceVerifier.read` had labeled this **claim-level insufficiency** as a `READER_ERROR:…` service failure. The pre-existing `verifyBatch` adaptive shortlisting logic **intentionally stops all subsequent batches on `READER_ERROR:`** to avoid multiplying network/timeout/HTTP/provider errors. The result: an empty model answer to source candidate A could prevent source candidate B from ever being considered, despite B being authorized, current and truly answer-bearing. This is a retrieval-availability regression independent of embedding/ranking and unrelated to response length.

**Single-owner safe fix** in the existing `packages/retrieval/src/evidence-reader.ts`: maintain the parser's fail-closed hard rejection, but map **only** `EVIDENCE_READER_REPLY_INCOMPLETE_ANSWER` at the passage-local `ReaderEvidenceVerifier.read` boundary to stable `INSUFFICIENT / READER_EXTRACTED_ANSWER_UNAVAILABLE`. Never admit the incomplete candidate and never invent an answer span. The adaptive shortlist can now advance to the next candidate and still enforce its original authorized quote→source-value→structural and relation policies. A timeout, provider/network error, invalid JSON or HTTP error remains `READER_ERROR:…` and **still stops further model calls**. No direct provider API payloads, system defaults, frozen annotations, score thresholds or writer-facing data changed.

**Contract tests:** new tests cover an incomplete first candidate followed by an independent second valid source (second source is read and can pass source citation and value verification), and two provider-failure variants that still stop further adaptive tranches without additional calls; original schema parsing still refuses incomplete replies. Focused reader/answer-value/admission **79/79 PASS**, typechecking and monorepo checks required prior to publication. This is a **structural recall opportunity**, not measured independent S2 semantic improvement: the full real HTTP/Source→Knowledge→ContextPacket→Writer test and genuinely new EN/ES source-family-disjoint role/polarity accuracy remain open. Keep **S2 OPEN, E5 SHADOW, S3–S5 BLOCKED**.

## 60. S2 — source-governed structured mismatch cannot fall back to model agreement (2026-10-26)

**Observed risk from the actual existing pipeline:** `ExactStructuredPropositionMatcher` is intentionally exact about asserted subject and predicate, and returns `INSUFFICIENT` for wrong/inverse roles. Yet the generic `LayeredEvidenceAdmissionPipeline.evaluateBatch` then calls the semantic reader on a structured mismatch. For a source-governed temporal fact, this lets an LLM generate `ANSWERS` to the inverse relation even when the persisted fact's source roles disagree. The previously observed historical false admission `sa-q33` (restaurant personnel notifies pest-control company, question reverses direction) illustrates the risk; this code change **does not claim to automatically extract this query's true roles**.

**Compatibility-preserving intervention:** add the explicit `structuredMismatchPolicy: "SEMANTIC_FALLBACK" | "ABSTAIN"` option to the _existing_ layered pipeline. `SEMANTIC_FALLBACK` remains the default, preserving exploratory search and the frozen family-disjoint benchmark's expected attribution semantics; no global model-source heuristics or ad-hoc language patterns were added. Only the `apps/api/src/governed-proposition-shadow.ts` path, after its existing authorization/scope/revision/source-bytes/projection checks, selects `"ABSTAIN"`. Under strict policy, a missing member of the structured pair returns `INSUFFICIENT / STRUCTURED_PROPOSITION_UNAVAILABLE`; a nonmatching explicit pair returns `INSUFFICIENT / STRUCTURED_PROPOSITION_UNRESOLVED`. Neither may call any model, including a model that proposes the opposite conclusion. Matching positive, object-mismatch and signed-polarity outcomes still pass through the original matcher and the structural source guard. An unsupported temporal/condition/modality qualifier continues to be rejected _before_ lookup by the governed shadow's existing rule.

**Proof obligation and limitation:** adversarial Retrieval tests show that an untrusted model `ANSWERS` on an inverse subject/destination can be admitted via the unchanged default exploratory fallback, while the same reversed and wrong-predicate structured inputs are rejected with **zero** model calls under the governed policy. A mixed batch shows both missing projections abstain while an exact pair remains admissible without a reader call. The authorized caller-provided `queryProposition` **is still an asserted input, not independently grounded from the natural-language question**. This is a formal _nonoverride_ guarantee after structured projection, not full semantic sufficiency, not an EN/ES entailment verifier, not new independent human-reviewed data and not an improvement to live Qwen answer-role precision. Full source-family-disjoint original-query role annotation and authenticated SOURCE→KNOWLEDGE→HTTP→ContextPacket→Writer per-claim citation adjudication remain mandatory. **S2 OPEN; E5 shadow; S3–S5 blocked; PR #38 draft/unmerged.**

## 61. S2 — governed original-query projection cannot use an unbound object as an answer wildcard (2026-10-26)

**Further formal gap in the strict shadow path:** `ExactStructuredPropositionMatcher` legitimately treats `StructuredPropositionQuery.object` as optional for generic exploratory use. When omitted, it compares only the supplied subject and predicate, allowing the candidate's source fact object to be arbitrary. The governed SOURCE→temporal-truth shadow must not call this a complete answer to an original free-text query that never established the requested object/value. The strict fallback veto from §60 prevents model overriding a mismatch but alone does not reject this _wildcard-positive_ match.

**Narrow fail-closed precondition:** `evaluateGovernedTemporalPropositionShadow` now requires a nonempty, trimmed `queryProposition.object`, in addition to its existing explicit polarity, unsupported-qualifier rejection, raw-source attestation and authorization filters. Missing/whitespace object returns zero evaluated/projected/accepted facts **before any DB/LLM call**. Generic exploratory matcher semantics, source facts, external APIs, thresholds, historical family-disjoint frozen oracle and default LAYERED status are unchanged. Added regression cases to the disposable PostgreSQL integration for both omitted and empty object, next to existing missing polarity, qualification and source-scope negatives.

**Caveat:** This only prevents a dangerously incomplete **caller-asserted typed projection** from being used as a match. It does not independently derive the object's value or role from the natural-language question. Such extraction and adjudication, plus signed independent original-source/claim/Writer acceptance and full request p95, remain S2 blockers. An optional same-model Qwen2.5 eight-facet diagnostic was then **completed** on a hash-pinned, post-hoc 48/48 subset of previously source-admitted `POLARITY` cases (24 EN and 24 ES, the known inverse `sa-q33` deliberately included). The second Qwen2.5 pass returned 21 `SUPPORTS`, 24 `INSUFFICIENT`, 3 `CONTRADICTS` and zero provider failures. It correctly rejected the inverse `sa-q33` with explicit direction/predicate/answerRole conflicts, but also vetoed **26/47 historically positive source units** (10/24 EN and 16/23 ES). Since source-unit positivity does **not** validate the proposed answer's meaning, these vetoes cannot automatically be called mistakes, nor can they be celebrated as precision gains. The same model generated the original answers and supplied the review: it is **not independent adjudication or source-family-blind holdout**. Raw ignored capture `.work/s2-qwen25-claim-facets-shadow-20261026/RAW_UNSCORED.json` SHA-256 `6ad25a615bb758a72a5a6af50425fc10ea918b29a9cdc5e8785f294b1e1a46d3`, post-scored only after 48/48 had completed. **No secondary model veto was enabled:** doing so would impose unmeasured extra model latency and could wrongly abstain on correctly supported claims. The existing F3/role-facet evaluation infrastructure remains the right place for independent review, not a new runtime heuristic. **S2 OPEN; E5 SHADOW; S3–S5 BLOCKED.**

## 62. S2 — 48/48 bilingual Qwen2.5 answer-role review and 127 source-bound answer adjudication packets (2026-10-26)

**Same-model eight-facet diagnostic COMPLETED; calibration only.** The real `qwen2.5:7b-instruct` re-read of 48 previously admitted `POLARITY` claims (24 EN, 24 ES, `sa-q33` deliberately selected) completed under pinned model digest `845dbda0ea48ed749caafd9e6037047aa19acfcfd82e704d7ca97d631a0b697e`, temperature 0 and strict structured-output, with zero provider failures. The second model saw only the historical question, the first-pass proposed answer and its exactly matched quote; developer gold was absent from its prompts. After capture the provisional label comparison gave **21 `SUPPORTS`, 24 `INSUFFICIENT`, 3 `CONTRADICTS`**, one negative source-unit veto (`sa-q33`, subject/predicate/direction reversal), **26 vetoes among 47 historically positive source units** (10/24 EN, 16/23 ES). Source-unit gold is not per-answer semantic gold; some vetoes are evidently warranted (e.g. `no` to frequency questions), while some appear unnecessarily strict (hours implying the library is open). **This is NOT new precision/recall against independently adjudicated answer correctness, and the second pass uses the SAME model family and is not independent corroboration.** Did not promote it to a production or shadow admission decision. Ignored local raw `.work/s2-qwen25-claim-facets-shadow-20261026/RAW_UNSCORED.json` SHA-256 `6ad25a615bb758a72a5a6af50425fc10ea918b29a9cdc5e8785f294b1e1a46d3`; exact subset and prompt are pinned by its `PLAN.json`. Do not tune the already-inspected heldout based on this outcome.

**Audit bottleneck identified:** the earlier **126/127 unit-label precision** was never independently adjudicated as _answer-correct precision_. Without fully judging the actual question, proposed answer, subject/destination, negation, condition, temporal scope and quote, a positive SOURCE unit cannot justify a factual claim. Rather than self-label the 127 admitted cases, implement an **offline-only dual-review preparation and structural auditor** in the existing Evaluation package (`packages/evaluation/src/s2-reader-claim-review.ts`), tested by `packages/evaluation/test/s2-reader-claim-review.test.ts` and invoked through `scripts/s2-prepare-reader-claim-review.ts` and `scripts/s2-audit-reader-claim-review.ts`. The generator re-loads the **entire completed 261-case raw v5 capture**, checks every question and source SHA-256 against the unchanged canonical pack, validates exact UTF-16 quote offsets and answer presence for each of the **127 actually admitted** records, and creates separate privately salted, shuffled X/Y packets containing _only_ the original question, proposed answer, cited source passage and exact quote. **Neither gold/source-unit label nor the model's admission/facet verdict is included.** The two packets are SHA-256 pinned in a receipt; reviewer X/Y skeletons begin unassigned and unresolved, with eight facets (subject, predicate, direction, polarity, modality, temporal, conditions, answerRole). The paired inspector detects altered receipts, quote spans, source text, mutated case rows, missing/duplicate labels, identical reviewer IDs and invalid facet enums, and can count disagreements **only after two complete self-attested submissions**.

**Executed output, status and custody:** `pnpm exec tsx scripts/s2-prepare-reader-claim-review.ts --capture .work/s2-261-qwen25-v5-20261026/RAW_UNSCORED.json --private-out .work/s2-reader-claim-review-20261026` → **127/127 packets created**; `pnpm exec tsx scripts/s2-audit-reader-claim-review.ts --private-dir .work/s2-reader-claim-review-20261026` → `PACKETS_VERIFIED_UNREVIEWED`, 127, independentHumanReviewVerified=false, releaseGateAccepted=false. All reviewer material and private response-key→case mapping are ignored under local `.work` and never committed. The source passage is the original selected `SearchHit.excerpt`, NOT a freshly authorized full immutable raw SOURCE read, and historical candidate selection used known answer-bearing units; the packet therefore does not claim blind source selection. Test fixtures use a reversed-direction example with emoji/UTF-16 boundary tampering and synthetic reviewer submissions **as structure-only tests**, never as real reviewer evidence. Standalone module tests, API/typechecks and full gates are recorded separately once completed.

**Hard remaining acceptance:** two genuinely independent external human reviewers, authenticated identity/separation, disagreements reconciled without moving gold, new source-family-disjoint EN/ES examples, correct query→claim structure, authenticated full SOURCE→KNOWLEDGE→QUERY→ContextPacket→Writer and end-to-end p95. Receipt hashing and reviewer self-attestation cannot authenticate actual human independence or establish truth. The audited answers are historical, not a prospective holdout. **S2 OPEN; E5 SHADOW; S3–S5 BLOCKED; PR #38 DRAFT/UNMERGED; LAYERED OFF by default.**

## 63. S2 — falsification of 99.2% answer precision; bounded source-literal recovery for EN/ES nonbinary answer slots (2026-10-26)

**Metric interpretation CORRECTED, with full 261/261 corpus audit rather than a tiny cherry-picked score.** The v5 Qwen2.5 raw capture frozen at SHA-256 `f9eabcb1610f9d9358cb0ed71904fcfed64ec830524931d0aacff47358e0d768` admitted **127** source-supplied candidates; **126** were attached to _historically labeled answer-bearing source units_, yielding the earlier **126/127 (99.2%) source-unit-label precision**. This is **not answer-correct precision** and not a score for retrieval+Writer. A deterministic, source-independent response-type audit on **all 127 original admitted questions and answers** (also independently reproducible from the two hash-pinned, private unadjudicated reviewer packets) identified **eight unambiguous wrong-form answers**: the model answered a _requested frequency, time or condition_ with a boolean `yes`/`no`. They occur **3 EN / 5 ES** and **3 development / 5 historically exposed heldout**. Examples: `How often is each engineer on call?` → `yes` despite the quotation `one week out of every six`; `¿Cuándo hay que cambiar el aceite de la freidora?` → `yes` despite a conditional threshold; `¿En qué casos no se puede renovar un préstamo?` → `no` despite a specified expired-loan case. These are answer-slot errors _even if the citation and source unit are correct_. Therefore even **ignoring every other type of semantic error**, original correct-answer precision **cannot exceed 119/127 = 93.70%**. The previously inspected, distinct inverse subject/destination false support `sa-q33` would tighten this bound to at most **118/127 = 92.91%** if counted as another established wrong claim; this historical case is not newly independent reviewer-adjudicated. Remaining source answers are **UNREVIEWED** for full claim correctness; these figures are mathematical **UPPER BOUNDS**, not point estimates or confidence intervals for full RAG. No existing developer labels, frozen oracle or holdout cases were changed.

**Reproducibility and privacy:** added `scripts/s2-audit-reader-answer-forms.ts --private-dir .work/s2-reader-claim-review-20261026`. It validates both private X/Y packet digests, exact common source/quote text and case set using the pre-existing review inspector (§62); audits every proposed answer via the general-purpose `explicitlyRequestsNonBooleanAnswer` classification; outputs only aggregate counts plus salted, irreversible response identifiers and `independentHumanAdjudication:false`. It never outputs private questions/source text, infers source-unit truth or marks the remaining 119 answers correct. The unversioned per-original-case explanatory audit is in `.work/s2-261-answer-form-audit-20261026/POSTRUN_AUDIT.json`. This is a **high-certainty falsification subset**, not a full independent precision evaluation.

**Source-level correction without source-specific heuristics:** new `packages/retrieval/src/question-answer-form.ts` conservatively recognizes unequivocally nonboolean question openings for frequency/time/condition/quantity/actor/place (EN/ES) without inspecting source content or introducing synonym/alias lists. In the **existing** `ReaderEvidenceVerifier.read`, the typed `POLARITY` path now returns `INSUFFICIENT / READER_ANSWER_FORM_INCOMPATIBLE_WITH_QUERY` for such a mismatch, **even when the quote is authentic and uniquely located**. A binary question, including conditional-led English/Spanish yes/no, remains unchanged. If and only if the **existing opt-in** `retryUnboundValueOnce:true` is active and this is the first read, allow exactly one existing `exactSourceValueOnly` retry on the **same query/source**; it must pass all pre-existing exact quote→UTF-16 source binding→typed literal→structural guard checks; invalid second attempt abstains with no loop. Existing API default `AKP_EVIDENCE_READER_RETRY_UNBOUND_VALUE_ONCE=false` remains untouched. There is no additional model, new regex over evidence contents, production LAYERED enablement or global claim entailment inference. Focused EN/ES classifier and reader tests include reject/one-retry/repeated-wrong-answer/binary and conditional-led controls; full quality gates remain required.

**Eight-case real Ollama falsification/recovery (NOT replayed or invented model decisions).** Re-read the exact eight previously admitted but definitively wrong-form source candidates with installed `qwen2.5:7b-instruct`, model digest `845dbda0ea48ed749caafd9e6037047aa19acfcfd82e704d7ca97d631a0b697e`, v5 strict literal-value instruction, real `ReaderEvidenceVerifier` and `StructuralEvidenceGuard`. The original source, query and model digest were SHA-pinned; no gold labels sent; **8/8 complete**, zero provider errors, **7/8 source-literal and guard-valid values**, **1/8 abstention**. Examples of replacement literal values: `one week out of every six`, `roughly every 600 flight hours`, `dos o más días`, `Los préstamos que ya están vencidos`. Per-candidate p50 **2.386 s**, p95 **11.955 s**, not full HTTP/Writer latency. Raw ignored capture `.work/s2-8-bad-form-literal-shadow-20261026/RAW_UNSCORED.json` SHA256 `5011d17d5e4e2dfb71c7a2b348cf98c22f559a967e2922dc193c9fa693d44221`. Exact literal source binding **does not independently prove full semantic condition/modality correctness**. The opt-in first-pass+retry control is separately proved by unit tests; this eight-case experiment forced the existing strict literal prompt once, not an authenticated live batch with two actual sequential reads.

**Separate publisher-annotated historical generality warnings, NOT pooled with the 261 scores:** previously archived XNLI EN/ES family-disjoint sample scored Qwen2.5 **11/24 entailments proposed supported and 2/48 negatives proposed supported**, and double-model agreement Qwen+Gemma **7/24 positives with 0/48 negatives**, indicating severe recall/safety tradeoffs. Those externally published labels are not freshly independent of possible model pretraining, and NLI entailment is not answer-generation accuracy. An older, historically captured 24-case scoped-HTTP four-family experiment admitted only **3/12 source-answerable cases** and **0/12 negatives** at retrieval; subsequent writer-level semantic correctness was NOT independently established. Its older commit/environment is not this HEAD. These historical measurements corroborate why supplied-source 99.2% cannot be sold as whole RAG precision and why retrieval rank/source authorization/Writer accuracy remain separate S2 gates.

**Remaining hard gate:** source-family-fresh, blinded human adjudication of all actual answer claims and independent query→source propositional roles (the two private packets remain `PACKETS_VERIFIED_UNREVIEWED`, not completed), new negative/adversarial questions EN/ES, ranking Recall@k, exact SOURCE→KNOWLEDGE scope→HTTP→ContextPacket→Writer per-claim support and actual p95 latency. **S2 OPEN · E5 SHADOW · S3–S5 BLOCKED · PR #38 DRAFT / UNMERGED · production LAYERED OFF**.

## 64. S2 — per-facet disagreement remains unresolved despite identical overall labels (2026-10-26; evaluation-only)

**Adversarial auditor gap, not a new semantic claim.** The existing historical X/Y submission inspector compared `answerFaithfulness` but did not compare the eight recorded relation facets. Two synthetic reviewers could both write `FULLY_SUPPORTED` while disagreeing on `direction`, `conditions` or `answerRole`; the report would previously count a fully supported consensus and zero unresolved cases. This is insufficient for the §63 goal of auditing _question → proposed answer → exact quote_, particularly for inverse directions and qualified negatives. The already-frozen 127 X/Y review packets remain unchanged and still have no human submissions.

**Narrow fix in the existing Evaluation owner:** `inspectS2ReaderClaimReviewSubmissions` now reports `facetDisagreementCases` and `facetDisagreementsByName` across all eight existing dimensions. A pair with identical top-level labels but any facet disagreement contributes to `unresolvedCases`; `bothSupported` and `bothWrongOrUnsupported` require matched labels **and** matched facets. A single reviewer claiming `FULLY_SUPPORTED` while simultaneously marking any facet `CONFLICT` or `UNKNOWN` is rejected as internally inconsistent; `NOT_APPLICABLE` remains available when a dimension genuinely does not apply. No source vocabulary, historical gold, model verdict, reader threshold, runtime search policy or citation was changed. These counts describe _internal review consistency_, not ground-truth correctness.

**Local proof:** the new deterministic negative regression tests same-label/different-direction, apparent full support with a contradictory or unknown facet, and same-label negative verdicts with divergent roles; passing agreement and existing packet-integrity tests remain. `@akp/evaluation` TypeScript typecheck PASS; its 59 test files / **444 tests PASS**; Prettier and `git diff --check` PASS on the two code/test files. These are local evaluation checks, not full monorepo or same-SHA CI of a published commit. The existing private 127-case packets independently returned `PACKETS_VERIFIED_UNREVIEWED`; no human reviewed them as part of this change.

**Gate unaffected:** human reviewer identities/separation and original SOURCE custody remain unauthenticated; no external human labels have been submitted. Historical 99.2% remains a source-unit metric, the eight unambiguous wrong-answer forms remain a **ceiling** proof only, and source-family-disjoint EN/ES claim correctness, false abstentions, full authorized retrieval→ContextPacket→Writer and end-to-end p95 remain **unproven**. S2 OPEN · E5 SHADOW · LAYERED OFF · S3–S5 BLOCKED · PR #38 DRAFT/UNMERGED.

## 65. S2 — reject genuine-quote laundering inside an altered HTTP excerpt (2026-10-26; evaluation-only)

**Confirmed red-before-green failure at SOURCE→HTTP→Writer diagnostics.** `replayS2RawHttpEvidence` already checked pinned raw HTTP request/response SHA-256, document/vault/revision/path, original whole-file SHA-256, exact UTF-16 quote coordinates, and whether the selected HTTP `hit.excerpt` _contained_ the quote. However, it did **not** prove that the _entire_ presented `hit.excerpt` originated from the same contiguous original SOURCE window. Two independently constructed negative regressions passed the old verifier as `REPLAY_VERIFIED` despite being ineligible: (1) a genuine cited substring surrounded by fabricated instruction-like text, with a newly consistent HTTP response pin; (2) an excerpt copied from the first of two source passages but a quote position anchored to the other occurrence. Both new tests initially **FAILED as expected** against the unmodified implementation (actual status `REPLAY_VERIFIED`), so this is a demonstrated proof-boundary defect rather than a conjecture.

**Single-owner defensive repair:** inside `packages/evaluation/src/s2-http-evidence-replay.ts`, the existing offline proof now checks that the complete literal `hit.excerpt` is a contiguous substring of the separately supplied, whole-byte-hash-verified original SOURCE **inside the declared body frame**, and that the exact cited UTF-16 `startUtf16/endUtf16` maps into the **same occurrence** of that excerpt. It fails closed with `HTTP_EXCERPT_NOT_ORIGINAL_SOURCE_SPAN` when any of these requirements is absent. This does not reconstruct or normalize source text, silently attach heading/title metadata, trust a source-alias string, or assign a fabricated original locator. An additional valid regression demonstrates that a shorter quote nested inside a larger _unaltered_ original excerpt (including a surrogate-pair emoji prefix) still replays; moving that excerpt outside the permitted original body frame fails. The two formerly false-positive regressions and all existing replay tests now pass (13/13 focused). The downstream `s2-end-to-end-evidence-trace` tests also passed (11/11).

**Strict-scope caveat:** a shaped Markdown/SECTION/parent-context presentation may not be contiguous with immutable raw SOURCE and will correctly fail this **literal offline proof** until a separately documented, independently verifiable extraction/structural-locator mapping exists. This does not change the current production API/search, ContextPacket, actual Writer, reader model, LAYERED/embedding flags, corpus gold or authorization policy. It does **not** imply that an original quote entails the question, that the authenticated caller has been cryptographically re-proven by this offline helper, or that all Writer statements cite their supporting raw SOURCE span.

**S2 release gate remains unchanged:** independently adjudicated, new-source-family EN/ES claim/negative/false-abstention accuracy; authenticated SOURCE→reviewed KNOWLEDGE→HTTP→ContextPacket→Writer statement-level evidence; source-cluster uncertainty and full request p95 are still outstanding. **S2 OPEN · E5 SHADOW · LAYERED OFF · S3–S5 BLOCKED · PR #38 DRAFT/UNMERGED.**

## 66. S2 — per-claim applicability cannot borrow coverage from a different claim (2026-10-27; evaluation-only)

**Reason for this change:** after reconciling all 106 PR #38 traceability comments and past R5/F3/F5/F6/E1–E6 model experiments, an existing E4 evaluator had a concrete untested multi-claim hole. `inspectS2SourceBoundRelationFacets` verified a source-bound, question/quote-hashed eight-facet review **per writer claim**, but counted `APPLICABLE` facets only in a single accumulator across the _whole answer_. A two-claim answer could be labeled `ALL_APPLICABLE_FACETS_ALIGNED_PROPOSAL_ONLY` even when all eight facets for an entire claim were `NOT_APPLICABLE`, because a different claim supplied the sole applicable checks. This permits an unreviewed condition, actor or answer role in one statement to borrow the appearance of complete review from another.

**Red-before-green falsification:** inserted an adversarial regression with two separately quoted SHA-verified claims from the existing source-bound `s2-complete-answer-evidence` fixture; one reviewer packet marks every facet of **one claim** `NOT_APPLICABLE`, while a different claim retains `subject`, `predicate`, and `answerRole` as `APPLICABLE/ALIGNED`. Before the fix, the actual evaluator returned **`COMPLETE_SUPPORTED_CANDIDATE`** and **`ALL_APPLICABLE_FACETS_ALIGNED_PROPOSAL_ONLY`** despite the unreviewed claim. The test failed as expected (18 PASS, 1 FAIL). Not synthetic evidence of a real model's semantic correctness: this is a pure evaluator invariant in a public deterministic fixture.

**Single-owner repair, no new verifier:** `packages/evaluation/src/s2-complete-answer-evidence.ts` now additionally requires **at least one explicitly APPLICABLE, source-bound reviewed facet _for each claim_**; otherwise `APPLICABILITY_REVIEW_REQUIRED`, and the enclosing assessment is `INSUFFICIENT` before invoking atomicity or consistency checks. The suite tests an uncovered claim in either position, independently of the correct source/claim hashes. Existing all-covered, relevant-UNKNOWN, observed-CONFLICT, whole-answer and quote attribution paths retain their behavior. This is a **minimum structural completeness condition**, not proof that even one applicable facet provides full entailment; reviewer origins, applicability correctness, semantic truth, negative FAR and answer role remain unverified.

**Validation at edit time:** targeted `s2-complete-answer-evidence` **19/19 PASS** after the code change, `@akp/evaluation` typecheck PASS, entire package **59 files / 448 tests PASS**, Prettier PASS and `git diff --check` PASS. Full repo gates and same-SHA CI are recorded separately when complete. No X/Y review packets, private originals, model outputs, author labels or frozen holdout changed, and no new model calls were needed for this directly reproducible bug.

**S2 remains OPEN.** This patch blocks only an evaluator coverage error. The next _real_ semantic experiment still needs independently reviewed, source-family-disjoint EN/ES natural-language question → complete claim → original-source proposition; both correct-support retention and false abstention, with genuine negative/inverse/exception/temporal cases, and whole authenticated original SOURCE→KNOWLEDGE→HTTP→ContextPacket→Writer. The already prepared 127 X/Y packets remain available for the owner's later independent review; never treat the unreviewed packets as ground truth. E5 SHADOW · LAYERED OFF by default · S3–S5 BLOCKED · PR #38 DRAFT/UNMERGED.

## 67. S2 — full-proposition Gemma3:4b paired DEV experiment on the historical 200 questions (2026-10-27; rejected, no policy change)

**Objective and provenance:** To test whether a distinct model reviewing the **whole natural-language question, proposed Writer answer and its historically source-bound citations** can block incorrect claims **without sacrificing correct answers**, we reused the private 200-task DEV set already documented in `docs/architecture/evidence-admission-research.md`. This is **not a new independently blind holdout**. The task families and previous model-authored semantic judgments were exposed before this trial. The archived writer was **not** regenerated, and the citations were the prior SHA-pinned quoted spans/source windows, not a fresh end-to-end rerun or renewed verification of all original SOURCE bytes. The model was `gemma3:4b`, fixed JSON-schema verdict `SUPPORTS | CONTRADICTS | INSUFFICIENT` plus eight proposition facets (subject, predicate, direction, polarity, modality, temporal, conditions, answerRole), temperature 0, seed 20261027, context 4096, output budget 170. No production configuration, admission model, thresholds or hidden labels were changed.

**Frozen before scoring:** The original 200 tasks were split into **100 English + 100 Spanish**, 58 existing affirmative Writer answers, 78 abstentions with one or more citations, and 64 no-admission tasks; 209 historical citations across the 136 tasks with evidence. All per-case model inputs excluded previous `reviewerVerdict`, `semanticVerdict` and other gold/review labels. The frozen prompt, original source-review files and case identities were hashed before any inference; model raw observations were persisted before the old DEV reviewer judgments were reopened for scoring. `200/200` task records, **136/136 valid Gemma model calls**, 64 deterministic no-evidence outcomes, **0** model errors or invalid responses. Every row was later rebound by `taskId`, language and original `questionSha256`. This makes the DEV _capture_ reproducible, but does **not** make the already-reviewed sample statistically independent.

**Primary unmodified arm — decisive rejection:**

| Historical DEV measure                         | Writer baseline | Proposed Gemma full-proposition veto |
| ---------------------------------------------- | --------------: | -----------------------------------: |
| Affirmative answers                            |              58 |                                   22 |
| Affirmatives reviewed CORRECT_SUPPORTED        |              44 |                                   15 |
| Affirmatives reviewed INCORRECT_UNSUPPORTED    |              14 |                                    7 |
| Descriptive answered precision                 |   44/58 = 75.9% |                        15/22 = 68.2% |
| Correct-answer retention against baseline      |    44/44 = 100% |                    15/44 = **34.1%** |
| Incorrect answers blocked relative to baseline |               — |                                 7/14 |
| Correct answers lost relative to baseline      |               — |                            **29/44** |

In English Gemma retained **7/23** reviewed correct answers and **2/6** reviewed incorrect; in Spanish **8/21** reviewed correct and **5/8** reviewed incorrect. Of 136 model calls: 112 `INSUFFICIENT`, 24 `SUPPORTS`, zero `CONTRADICTS`. Twenty-two `SUPPORTS` belonged to already affirmative Writer outputs; two to abstentions with citations, which are **only source-rescue hypotheses, not generated/correct answers**. None of the 64 no-citation tasks was declared a justified negative. Observed **per-candidate Gemma p50 6.639 s, p95 22.871 s, maximum 66.247 s**; this is neither Writer+verification latency nor complete HTTP p95. The historical Qwen claim-veto replay on the same DEV sample retained **32/44 correct** while blocking 7/14 incorrect; Gemma's **15/44** retention is substantially worse. Both are **REJECTED** as mandatory vetoes, not independently accepted model-quality estimates.

**Separate, posthoc facet-consistency audit (do not reinterpret as the preregistered arm):** Of Gemma's 24 raw `SUPPORTS` declarations, **12 simultaneously declared at least one `CONFLICT` facet**, two had at least one `UNKNOWN` facet, and three labeled **all eight facets `NOT_APPLICABLE`**. Only **7/24** support declarations were even structurally coherent (all facets ALIGNED/NOT_APPLICABLE, with at least one ALIGNED). The isolated, read-only _sensitivity analysis_ requiring this minimum self-consistency would retain **7/58** historical affirmative answers, of which **3/44 correct** and **4/14 incorrect** according to the historical model-generated reviewer labels (EN: 3 correct/2 wrong; ES: 0 correct/2 wrong). It loses 41/44 previously correct answers and therefore makes the veto worse. Both positive `SOURCE_ANSWERABILITY` proposals also fail this self-consistency filter. This is **not** a second experiment, semantic truth check, or valid new threshold: it is a direct internal-inconsistency diagnosis of already-frozen responses. Even a coherent facet set would not independently prove entailment.

**Private SHA-256 receipts (never commit raw cases, prompts, originals or model outputs):**

- Frozen 200-case input projection: `869c2b0d1db23e7271527f823b70bbf3b1eaf79b3c1153b3cfd7fb407096ce4c`.
- Pre-inference manifest `.work/s2-proposition-ab-200-20261027/FREEZE.json`: `3f8049bd43a8de965121f5b3c365acbef412a764763f21e7e4199470be7ea4d8`.
- Completed raw unscored `RAW_UNSCORED.jsonl`: `b65ee45002432e8bcb4237c77a972b61dae70c8914fbf923b84f0d4b356de877`.
- Primary `SCORE_PRIVATE.json`: `e7c77a8923f0cdc5a08844894b6f4b78e390de29c113719dd9b425452bb968c6`.
- Paired per-case `PAIR_PRIVATE.jsonl`: `c68489edcbf673169345bc3b12712b9f8c80702fe3bdd3b43e0c9c3281dce2c1`.
- Posthoc self-consistency `FACET_CONSISTENCY_POSTHOC.json`: `6930536f71191bd334fb711c77315b477a5d18c5187ea5b6b42c67b30cfe1549`.

The private runner and audit live **only in ignored `.work`**, not in committed app code. The posthoc audit confirmed unique joins for 200 tasks, frozen manifest/input hash linkage, all historical input/source-file hashes, 136 valid calls, 64 no-source outcomes and 58 historic Writer review IDs. The historical writer reviewer/reference was itself **AI-adjudicated and previously inspected**; reported correct/incorrect labels are provisional. `SUPPORTS` is a model proposal, not an independently verified atomic answer. No statement-level original SOURCE+reviewed KNOWLEDGE+authenticated HTTP+ContextPacket+Writer measurement was established by this offline replay.

**Decision:** `REJECT_GEMMA_FULL_PROPOSITION_MANDATORY_VETO`. Do not add another default model judge, tune the verdict threshold on this exposed DEV corpus, rerun the same 200 questions as a blind holdout, promote E5, enable LAYERED/retry, or quietly treat empty evidence as justified abstention. The causal priority remains to distinguish **(i)** missing/incorrect candidate or source unit, **(ii)** insufficient qualifying SOURCE spans, and **(iii)** Writer misuse of already sufficient evidence, then preregister a **new source-/publisher-/relation-family-disjoint EN/ES** paired experiment with independently adjudicated all-question/all-claim accuracy, correct-positive retention, wrong-support/FAR, necessary-vs-unnecessary abstentions, original citation provenance, full authenticated Writer runtime and cost. The user's separate 127 X/Y reviewer packages remain **untouched and unreviewed**. **S2 OPEN · E5 SHADOW · LAYERED OFF · S3–S5 BLOCKED · PR #38 DRAFT/UNMERGED.**

## 68. S2 — causal stage-attribution of the complete historical 200-query pipeline (2026-10-27; DEV diagnostic, no product change)

**Question:** where are the answerable cases lost between original SOURCE, ranked candidate selection, Qwen LAYERED evidence admission, citation semantic sufficiency and the historical Writer? After the rejected §67 Gemma veto, the priority is to investigate _loss location_, not stack another model judge. This section is based entirely on **read-only, SHA-pinned existing per-case receipts**, including **200 archived authenticated HTTP responses and admission shadow traces**, 200 historical Writer outcomes, 209 cited spans, 200 provisionally adjudicated EN/ES questions, and original Markdown bytes for all **34 source families** in the isolated historical vault.
