New offline auditors and receipts are private in ignored `.work`. No new model call, current production HTTP re-execution, X/Y judgment, model threshold change or source mutation was performed.

**Source and join verification:** 200 unique task IDs, exact language/question SHA-256 joins; 126 provisionally answerable (EN 65 / ES 61), 66 provisionally unanswerable and 8 ambiguous. All **126/126 positive gold passages** match a SHA-256 verified original among 34 Markdown files, and the reference quotes replay at their declared original UTF-16 offsets. In the HTTP trace, all 34 SOURCE document paths were recovered from visible hit identities; mapping SHA-256 of their actual document UUIDs to the shadow candidate `documentIdSha256` (rather than treating ten exploratory hits as the complete candidate set) gives **126/126 gold documents represented among the 64 reader candidates** (EN 65/65; ES 61/61). This is **document availability, not answer-bearing unit recall or independent question sufficiency**. Only **74/126** gold documents were visible in the separate `exploratoryHits` top-ten field (EN 42/65; ES 32/61), yet **101/126** were present among admitted `hits` (EN 49; ES 52); thus the exploratory top ten cannot be used to blame upstream retrieval.

**Exhaustive and mutually exclusive classification of the 126 positive cases:**

| Pipeline stage / historical outcome                                                             |     EN |     ES |     All |
| ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -----: | -----: | ------: |
| Gold SOURCE document among 64 candidates but not admitted, **no alternate sufficient citation** |     14 |      8 |  **22** |
| Gold document admitted, but **no citation judged sufficient**                                   |     15 |     12 |  **27** |
| At least one citation judged sufficient, Writer abstained                                       |     12 |     19 |  **31** |
| At least one such citation, Writer output judged incorrect                                      |      1 |      1 |   **2** |
| At least one such citation, invalid/degraded final result                                       |      1 |      0 |   **1** |
| Final response judged correct by historical AI reviewer                                         |     22 |     21 |  **43** |
| **Positive denominator**                                                                        | **65** | **61** | **126** |

The first two rows are **49/126** positives without a citation judged semantically answering: **18** had zero admitted citations and **31** had cited text but no reviewer-judged sufficient span. In **25/126** the gold document itself was not admitted, but three of those questions had _alternative citations judged sufficient_, so only **22** appear in the exclusive first row. Conversely **77/126** had at least one citation judged sufficient, split 43 correct, 31 abstained, 2 wrong and 1 degraded; a **single sufficient citation might still fail to answer a compound question**, so none of these counts independently proves full-question answerability.

**Reader/admission causal detail:** Across the **22** lost cases in the first row, **57 candidate units** belonged to the original gold document. Candidate-level reasons were **31 `LAYERED_ADMISSION_OUTSIDE_BOUNDED_WINDOW`**, 6 `NOT_SHORTLISTED_FOR_READING`, 13 `READER_FOUND_NO_ANSWER`, 4 `READER_QUOTE_NOT_IN_PASSAGE`, 2 `STRUCTURAL_REQUIRED_FACT_MISSING`, 1 reader response invalid (reason counts are **units**, not independent cases). Despite skipped units, **19/22 cases had at least one gold-document unit actually read and judged insufficient**; only two had **no** gold-document unit verified. The missing citations therefore cannot be solved merely by increasing the 16-of-64 bounded reading window. It would raise latency and leaves question/answer-role interpretation untouched. Among the **27** second-row cases the same reader marked **28** gold-document candidate units `READER_QUOTED_ANSWER`/SUPPORTS yet no resulting citation was judged semantically sufficient by the historical direct reviewer. This is a measured **reader↔semantic reviewer disagreement**, not evidence the reviewer is correct.

**Original quote vs passage selection:** Exact source-gold UTF-16 positions were compared with the **fully replayed original-source coordinates** of the historical 209 citations. Among 31 positive tasks with citations but none judged sufficient, **23** still had a cited source span overlapping a provisionally gold answer span (and the complete citation passage contained the same gold quote). Among the 31 Writer abstentions despite at least one judged sufficient citation, **28** had citation passages containing the exact gold quote; **28** had direct original-source position overlap. The problem is often **qualification/use of available answer text**, not just retrieving the wrong file or missing a literal answer. These are checks of literal correspondence, **not semantic entailment or human adjudication**.

**Historical citation-coordinate contract, important caveat:** All **209/209** reviewer citations have matching original document SHA-256, quote SHA-256, a literal whole passage occurring **exactly once** in that original SOURCE, and an exact UTF-16 `[start,end)` slice **relative to that passage**. **0/209** reviewer offsets are already absolute raw SOURCE coordinates. A private proof safely reconstructed **209/209 absolute original UTF-16 spans** as `original.indexOf(uniquePassage) + passageRelativeStart` and rechecked quote bytes; this must not be confused with simply passing historical passage-relative offsets to the runtime's SOURCE-absolute citation contract. Earlier preliminary diagnostics directly comparing these two coordinate systems produced false failures and are **superseded by this verified rebinding**. No runtime citation rewrite was necessary or made.

**Negatives and confidence:** Among 66 provisionally negative questions, 26 received at least one citation, five had some citation that a distinct historical AI reviewer called semantically answering **despite the negative provisional category**, and five yielded a Writer answer (two intersect those reviewer-disagreement cases). These are review conflicts or possible false support requiring _independent_ adjudication; never claim measured production FAR from this exposed DEV set. The 49 positive pre-sufficiency losses span **24 of 34 source families**; the 31 positive abstentions with some judged sufficient citation span **20 families**, so a single-template fix or simple binomial interval is inappropriate.

**Latency and other existing experiments:** Archived 200-case HTTP Search p50 **18.503s**, p95 **23.563s**; admission-component p95 **19.874s**; historical Writer-component p95 **2.345s**. The p95 of **paired per-case sums** (`HTTPelapsed + Writerelapsed`) is **25.194s** overall (EN 25.491s, ES 25.083s): this is a **two-stage historical approximation, not actual fresh authenticated total request p95**, and no percentile should be summed directly. It is already marginal versus provisional ≤25s budget. Prior historical QA-hint rescue on 17 abstentions emitted seven answers but only three passed direct full-answer/language judgment; **four were incomplete/noncompliant, so that rescue was rejected**. §67's Gemma and the prior Qwen veto lost correct answers. Repeating those schemes or raising reader-window work without an explicit time/coverage hypothesis is unsupported.

**What is actually demonstrated:** On this exposed historical dataset, _document-family recall into the 64-unit reader pool was not the primary failure_; first-stage losses concentrate in choosing/accepting a **semantically adequate passage** (49 positives), followed by **answer production/use of potentially sufficient evidence** (31 positive abstentions). Even exact gold quote overlap and source hashes do not prove the complete proposition (subject, predicate, direction, negation, modality, time, conditions, full answer role). Every quality label remains previously AI-reviewed and source/question-family correlated; this does not close any independent S2 gate.

**Next controlled experiment, with explicit causal separation (not run by this read-only audit):** freeze genuinely fresh, publisher/source/relation-family-disjoint EN/ES queries and original gold answer spans before inference; record all 64 candidate **unit** ranks and source hashes, per-unit Qwen judgment/reason and bounded-window positions, admitted exact raw SOURCE spans, Writer statement-level claims and precise citations. Run **one variable at a time**: compare the same original ranked pool's evidence-unit selection and source-qualification policy while preserving authorization/revision, and separately compare the existing Writer vs one source-grounded answer-completion strategy on the **same admissible original spans**, with equivalent budgets. Measure correct answer/claim retention, false support, complete-question answerability, unnecessary abstentions, and full cold/warm HTTP p95; only subsequently consult independent human source/claim review, including the owner's pending X/Y review. No model second-vote veto, no oracle-based production ranking, no global numerical-table ban, and no LAYERED/retry promotion.

**Private artifact SHA-256:** `SHADOW_AND_CITATION_PRIVATE.json` `4d75efc15584d60974f28b784675baab8adb9947f2a141947f82aa53e389c402`; `GOLD_READER_CAUSAL_PRIVATE.json` `85ae7c5891956d772f160fdfa0b2d64b98a5d7a62af0bb4dca2323514073b19b`; `SOURCE_CITATIONS_REBOUND_PRIVATE.jsonl` `30660b1fdfd6d3def5fc2af2bcf8fdf6293a42eae2065e426b5623918608c332`; `SOURCE_QUOTE_VS_WRITER_STAGE_PRIVATE.json` `fa3c1533a0a1b85bde07dd8596e30c300e01d075c2d7346d741a2402c6c25185`; `GOLD_READER_WINDOW_LOSS_PRIVATE.json` `19fcba0721fcab893e2206bcc9c3ac574c799f54058d63e8e7dab85284c41838`. These are **private, ignored** receipts and not public validation of original source ownership.

**Release and publication:** E5 SHADOW; S2 OPEN; LAYERED OFF; S3–S5 BLOCKED; PR #38 DRAFT/UNMERGED. §67 remains committed only in the local worktree (remote Git tree creation was blocked by safety controls); this section will stay local and the non-sensitive summary will be placed in a PR comment. Do not claim remote CI for an unpublished local commit.

## 69. S2 — v5 reader oracle-window diagnostic on 49 lost positives and 26 admitted negatives (2026-10-27; experiment completed, no policy change)

**Causal purpose:** The §68 200-question pipeline replay found all **126/126** positive reference SOURCE documents within the actual 64 LAYERED candidates but **49/126** with no citation judged sufficient; 31 more abstained despite some reviewer-judged sufficient citation. We tested the narrower question: **when the v5 reader directly sees an authentic original passage containing a reference answer span, can it extract an answer and literal cite?** This is **not** another model-based veto, answer correctness measure, independent holdout, or viable oracle retrieval policy.

**Frozen design and custody:** On the same previously inspected 200-case/34-source DEV collection, freeze **49** positives lacking any reviewer-judged sufficient admitted citation (EN 29, ES 20) and **26** provisional negatives that nevertheless had prior admitted citations (EN 13, ES 13). For each positive, a deterministic oracle selected **the longest existing reference gold span** and an unchanged original SOURCE window including 240 UTF-16 characters of prefix and 280 of suffix; the SHA-256 and quote boundaries were checked against original historical Markdown bytes. For each negative, a deterministic rule selected the longest _actually admitted_ literal original citation passage, not an oracle positive. Hence **the positive/negative input-selection policies differ**; comparing their answer rates does not measure FAR, precision or any deployable threshold. Multi-span/compound questions may require more than one original span; the intervention supplies only one.

**Actual reader:** local `qwen2.5:7b-instruct` through Ollama `/api/chat`, with unchanged repository `evidenceReaderMessages` **v5** prompt, repository JSON response schema and parser, temperature 0, seed 20261027, 4096 context tokens, output budget 360; one call per frozen task. Replayed the literal `answer_span` against its provided SOURCE window and rejected bare yes/no on unambiguously nonboolean questions in the observation evaluator. **This is not the complete `ReaderEvidenceVerifier` admission path** (e.g. unique extracted-value rebinding and full candidate authorization are not rerun), nor a Writer. The judgement `ANSWERS` with a valid literal quote is a structural answer-shaped observation, **not proof that subject, agent/recipient, negation, modality, conditions, time or all requested facts are correct**.

**Execution:** **75/75** model responses captured and pinned before scoring, **0 invalid responses or provider failures**. Original input case manifest SHA-256 `08e6fd4357803873e3da5dd03391af2188b1c8d27a33443e46694457901a88e5`; case input SHA `967fc11c932556fc3a0fdd7c3090e3a0800a7d5282b714f452abb75acc89c042`; unscored raw SHA `b23842adae850beddb3d5f4c27ea636f59eede3944235d6e18d2ccf54483d823`.

| v5 observation on single authentic original SOURCE window              | Answer verdict | Quote literal valid / answer-shaped | Declined | Answer span NOT literal |
| ---------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------: | ----------------------------------: | -------: | ----------------------: |
| 49 **oracle-selected positive** windows (EN 29, ES 20)                 |             30 |                           **24/49** |       19 |                       6 |
| Of those, **22 gold documents not admitted/no sufficient** in baseline |              9 |                            **6/22** |       13 |                       3 |
| Of those, **27 gold documents admitted/no sufficient** in baseline     |             21 |                           **18/27** |        6 |                       3 |
| 26 **actually admitted negative-control** passages (EN 13, ES 13)      |             18 |                           **17/26** |        8 |                       1 |

EN positive **11/29** answer-shaped/source-literal, ES positive **13/20**. Of 38 positive tasks with a **single reference span**, 20 returned an answer-shaped literal cite; of 11 with multiple reference spans, only four. EN negative-control **7/13** answer-shaped, ES negative-control **10/13**; these 17 are **not automatically wrong answers**: explicit negative source evidence can correctly answer some binary propositions, and the prior negative categories were produced by AI rather than independent human adjudication. Of 26 negative controls, five contained citations the earlier reviewer independently marked `ANSWERS`; four of those five are among the 17 literal v5 answer-shaped outcomes. Do not silently reconcile model-review disagreement.

**Loss attribution:** With a gold-bearing passage handed to it, v5 still declined **19/49** positive requests and produced **six additional nonliteral answer spans**; simply widening original document recall cannot remove these issues. On the 27 **gold-document-admitted but semantically insufficient** cases, the model generated a literal answer-shaped quote for **18/27** with a different, oracle-positioned input. This points toward **source unit / source-window construction, full relation attribution or reviewer disagreement**, which must be tested independently case-by-case before selecting a remedy. The 22 first-stage rejected cases reached only **6/22** source-literal answer shapes even with the gold window; indiscriminately expanding the reader shortlist is not an evidence-based fix. Eight-facet, original-source human adjudication is still required to distinguish a plausible quote from genuinely sufficient proposition entailment.

**Cost:** per-reader model-call p95 **5.021s** overall (positive p95 5.021s, negative p95 3.936s), **not a full HTTP or Writer p95**. §68's paired historical HTTP + Writer p95 proxy was already ~**25.194s**. An additional mandatory reader verification would jeopardize that latency target absent proven coverage benefit.

**Immutable private receipts:** `.work/s2-oracle-v5-stage200-20261027/FREEZE.json` `08e6fd4357803873e3da5dd03391af2188b1c8d27a33443e46694457901a88e5`; `INPUTS_UNSCORED_PRIVATE.json` `967fc11c932556fc3a0fdd7c3090e3a0800a7d5282b714f452abb75acc89c042`; `QWEN_V5_RAW_UNSCORED.jsonl` `b23842adae850beddb3d5f4c27ea636f59eede3944235d6e18d2ccf54483d823`; `SCORE_PRIVATE.json` `84a1914616361b6644d88fec3cb146b80cf4fa35478c43149fdc4a7b6c7b1e77`; `CAUSAL_JOIN_PRIVATE.json` `90adc80ed6f0fc092b6ee4ddd432b2cec84ffb768f383fa42b42472a5810e01c`. Raw questions, gold quotes, original source passages, model replies and exact case IDs remain private in ignored `.work`; no reviewer submissions were created for the owner's separate X/Y packets.

**Decision and next genuinely new controlled slice:** `NO_PROMOTION`. Do not turn this oracle-biased result into source ranking, another LLM veto, a supposed 49% semantic success rate or a negative FAR. Future independent EN/ES SOURCE-families must freeze actual **answer-bearing unit identities**, not only gold document paths, and compare **same candidate windows with only one factor changed** (unit selection versus relation interpretation), then use **exact same current authorized admitted SOURCE spans** for a separate Writer-completion paired A/B. Track atomic claim entailment, false support, retained true positives, unnecessary abstentions and actual end-to-end p95 by source cluster. Maintain S2 OPEN, E5 SHADOW, LAYERED and retry defaults OFF, S3–S5 BLOCKED and PR #38 DRAFT/UNMERGED; §67–§69 documentation is local until publishing is authorized.

## 70. S2 — paired Writer same-admitted-source answer-slot prompting, 80 DEV questions (2026-10-27; experiment rejected for release)

**Motivation and falsifiable hypothesis:** §68 located **31 positive abstentions despite at least one citation previously judged semantically answering** by a historical AI reviewer. §69 found that even an oracle source window does not make v5 a reliable complete-claim verifier. We therefore tested whether one different instruction to **the Writer itself** (explicitly checking all requested answer slots, relation direction, negation and conditions) could recover otherwise missing answers **with the exact same admitted source excerpts**, without a second LLM veto, QA model proposal, candidate-count change or gold answer injection.

**Pairing and frozen inputs:** **80** historical, previously inspected DEV tasks with source citations: 31 positive `ABSTAIN`+some reviewer-sufficient citation, nine positive historically AI-reviewed incorrect affirmative responses, 14 historically AI-reviewed correct affirmative controls (seven EN and seven ES selected by SHA-256 order of task ID), and 26 provisionally negative questions that nevertheless had admitted citations. Original question IDs, question SHA-256, `SRC` identifiers and literal admitted quotes were checked against the 200-task historical writer-v1 inputs and writer-v2 provenance, and frozen before capture. Previous answers, original gold/reference spans, and reviewer labels were **not** included in the model prompts. The cohorts were, however, selected using already exposed DEV references, so no holdout independence can be claimed.

**One-variable experimental setup:** Both paired arms used the **same Qwen2.5:7b-instruct**, temperature 0, seed 1317, 160 max output tokens, same OpenAI-compatible `/v1/chat/completions` endpoint, same strict three-field `ANSWER|ABSTAIN` JSON schema, same question and **byte-identical historical `SRC` quotes**. `BASELINE_V2` was the exact historically recorded original writer system prompt, rerun in the current environment rather than compared only against an older response. `ANSWER_SLOTS` changed **only the system instruction** to enumerate the question's subject, actor/receiver direction, polarity, time, conditions and all requested slots internally before answering. Neither arm received any per-question answer hint. The output auditor verified schema, nonempty answer/citations or well-formed abstention and that every cited ID belonged to its supplied excerpts. Model raw answers/identities were saved privately; passing a citation-ID check **does not prove semantic support**.

**Capture integrity:** **160/160** completed records, **80 complete same-input pairs**, **160/160 structurally valid schema and cited IDs**, zero model/provider errors in the accepted capture. An _earlier experimental recording error_ (`citationValid` vs `citationsValid` variable) caused 31 successful provider replies to be mislogged `PROVIDER_ERROR` before it was detected; that pilot was **stopped** and preserved in a different ignored private directory, and **none of its rows entered scoring**. The freeze was copied byte-for-byte to a clean v2 capture directory; the script variable name alone was repaired before restarting. Frozen input/model settings were unchanged. Do not characterize this local auditor typo as a product/Writer failure.

**Paired observed transitions (baseline rerun vs answer-slot instruction):**

| Prior reviewed DEV cohort                                                     |  Count | Baseline ANSWER | Variant ANSWER | ABSTAIN→ANSWER | ANSWER→ABSTAIN |
| ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -----: | --------------: | -------------: | -------------: | -------------: |
| Positive with previously sufficient citation, baseline historically abstained |     31 |               2 |         **16** |         **14** |              0 |
| Positive previously reviewed **incorrect** answer                             |      9 |               9 |              8 |              0 |              1 |
| Positive previously reviewed correct control                                  |     14 |              14 |             14 |              0 |              0 |
| Provisional negative with admitted citations                                  |     26 |               6 |         **13** |          **7** |              0 |
| **All**                                                                       | **80** |          **31** |         **51** |         **21** |          **1** |

Original historical abstention is not identical to a _rerun_ under the same model; two of the first 31 now answered in the baseline arm without any variant, and were **not** counted as variant recoveries. The current A/B has **14 additional affirmative candidate answers** in the lost-positive stratum, but **7 additional answers in the negative controls**, and 45 changed answer strings across the 80 cases (including ten of fourteen historically correct controls). Language-level net answer increase EN 16→21, ES 15→30. Local per-Writer-call p95 baseline **3.200s** vs variant **2.508s**; this is **not** end-to-end request p95, nor a guaranteed efficiency improvement.

**Qualitative source/claim red flags observed in the _seven_ newly answered negative controls:** a question requesting the _specific page or section number_ was answered with the generic phrase "página o sección exacta", not any number; a question asking _which architectural pattern requires no rollback_ was answered with "orden" (order), which is not a pattern and the cited text did not settle the required exception; a question asking whether repository architecture decisions were _undocumented_ received "no" from an instruction to link architecture decisions in the vault, which is not evidence that all decisions are documented. A request for a non-university profile and its authority relationship was answered only with the profile category rather than identifying it or explaining the required relation. In newly answered positive cases, some proposed answers similarly repeated "the relevant contract", "IStringLocalizer" or "the active and corrected university profile" without filling the requested role or identity. These are inspectable answer-form/entailment failure examples from a **non-independent internal diagnostic**, not adjudicated individual precision labels. They falsify the inference that 21 extra validly cited answer emissions equal 21 correct recoveries.

**Decision and scope:** **REJECT_ANSWER_SLOTS_AS_DEFAULT_WRITER_PROMPT** on this evidence; _candidate recovery_ is not demonstrated _semantically correct recovery_. Do not treat all 26 provisional negative cases as necessarily false answers: an explicit denial can be correctly source-grounded, and the historical negative/reference and citation judgments conflict in places. Do not derive production FAR from this development trial. The experiment establishes that a prompt can shift the Writer's abstention policy at unchanged source/latency budget, but it may do so unsafely. **No production Reader/Writer runtime, flags, ACL, SOURCE, gold labels or X/Y packets changed.**

**Private receipt SHA-256 (ignored `.work`):** clean v2 `FREEZE.json` `bff3c901b067aea0213c611cd5e2e5154728d2c13441b9d32ba583d7c711ace1`; `INPUTS_UNSCORED_PRIVATE.json` `209ecad42eba81d37725d55102d999ef07e2fcd1aea5db3dd550a910415d9a7d`; `RAW_UNSCORED.jsonl` `81096b5855e8f863e9daa27c78b11ee6eadda78388c5179782d141f632842d0f`; `PAIRS_PRIVATE.jsonl` `1277ac06520f97abc8fa2d6aef07ca1137131f4c250bef76e1e64a1bbf9837d5`; `SCORE_PRIVATE.json` `47d304d45e5fc12a07fd2761e13ad5e60af617cd836026402201d8637d52c884`. The superseded failed pilot raw file is retained separately with SHA `bd120be0d44f26f4b68d903c68001714bdec6cf8206b5bbd7d294268453d195c` and must not be incorporated as model failure cases or into observed transition denominators.

**Next stage to resolve before S2 acceptance:** Independently inspect _atomic claim and question-slot support_ for paired Writer emissions using newly sourced, publisher/source-/relation-family-disjoint EN/ES documents; when the writer abstains, distinguish insufficient evidence from failure to utilize adequate evidence. Preserve actual original SOURCE offsets, authorized current revisions, exact source-unit selection and full HTTP ContextPacket→Writer custody; evaluate nonbinary answer forms, reversed relations, conditional exceptions, correct-positive retention and false support with source-family-clustered confidence intervals and whole-request cold/warm latency. Neither the QA hint, Gemma veto, generalized reader-window expansion nor this answer-slot prompt has evidence sufficient for default promotion. S2 OPEN, E5 SHADOW, LAYERED OFF, S3–S5 BLOCKED, PR #38 OPEN/DRAFT/UNMERGED.

## 71. S2 — real admitted excerpt versus oracle-centered original SOURCE, paired v5 Reader (2026-10-27; evidence-selection hypothesis rejected)

**Motivation:** The source-by-source analysis of §68 showed document-level candidates existed for 126/126 author-positive questions, but 49 produced no historical citation judged semantically sufficient. §69 gave Qwen v5 directly a gold-centered source window for those 49, recovering answer-shaped literal quotes only in 24/49. This experiment isolates **whether centering the Reader input around the known gold substring is enough to explain a loss** when the original document's actual HTTP admitted excerpt is available.

**Source and cohort:** Rejoined all 49 historical DEV-labeled lost-positive cases by exact task and original question SHA. Chose the **single longest source-verified gold quote for each** as a reproducible, explicitly **oracle-biased** reference; this is not every possible sufficient alternative. Its specific original SOURCE document appeared in the actual historical HTTP admitted hits for **25/49** (not equivalent to §68's 27 cases with _some_ gold-document admission: the chosen span's document can differ from another gold-reference document). The admitted excerpt contains that selected exact gold quote in **20/25**; only five of those 25 lack its literal text. This already argues against the blanket assumption that all reference units are missing from the admission output. These documents are source-hash matched, but an admitted hit's shaped excerpt is not automatically an independently replayed full unmodified SOURCE substring.

**Identical-model paired test:** Freeze exactly 25 questions and source/hash pointers, then compare `ACTUAL_ADMITTED` (first admitted excerpt from the selected original document, **not gold-selected within that document**) against `ORACLE_ORIGINAL` (original-byte gold-centered window from §69). The only factor in the two arms is the Reader input passage; both use actual Qwen2.5:7b-instruct, the repository's unchanged v5 evidence Reader prompt, parser and literal-quote check, temperature 0, seed 20261027, 4096 context and 360 output tokens. Nothing enters production. **50/50** valid model responses; zero invalid/provider errors **in the accepted run**.

| Group                                                         | Real admitted excerpt: answer-shaped literal | Gold-centered original window: answer-shaped literal |
| ------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------: | ---------------------------------------------------: |
| **All 25** paired lost positives                              |                                    **19/25** |                                            **17/25** |
| Gold quote already literal inside real excerpt (**20** cases) |                                    **17/20** |                                            **14/20** |
| Gold quote absent from the first real excerpt (**5** cases)   |                                          2/5 |                                                  3/5 |

Of the 25, **14** were answer-shaped in both arms; **5** only from the admitted excerpt; **3** only from the gold-centered original; and **3** from neither. English: 10/13 admitted vs 8/13 oracle; Spanish: 9/12 for both. Hence **oracle centering did not improve v5 literal response extraction on this paired subset**. The Qwen's answer-shaped quote is not proof of the **full subject/action/date/condition or answer role**; the prior reviewer label (none sufficient) may also be mistaken. This is a _source-window sensitivity_ experiment, **not independent semantic precision** or a reason to enable automatic citation admission.

**Operational failure isolated, not scored:** Ollama was initially offline; the first 50 attempted calls all failed `fetch failed` in a separate ignored private capture. They were never scored as Reader semantics. Restored local Ollama server and replayed the **byte-identical SHA-256 frozen cohort** into a clean v2 output; accepted 50 responses all valid. The original failed capture remains separate for traceability. No inference result, prompt, source or reference was rewritten after scoring.

**Private SHA-256:** `ADMITTED_UNIT_GOLD_AUDIT.json` `5fac64f2572dcb6b58028f22da90497f8709f84c14ed19e030772d387c796f7c`; accepted `PAIR_FROZEN.json` `dabc7c7cb1392e2cde55016d2922ee5a22a2e2645145b36837d0e30bf04c684a`; `PAIR_QWEN_RAW_UNSCORED.jsonl` `88f273b9f6440a0712eeb9c5c981c9ef85f6b063061c1f95de598415f2304686`; `PAIR_SCORE_PRIVATE.json` `38c4ad9bb46b001ab9c5b645b6daac7304a8dda0f44149daa4d827ad078b981f`; rejected offline-provider raw `cbd97f37d4d5e9f72fc858898f585b8d5f76063ea6d7ff3910109d0e730fa9f8`. Every raw question and excerpt remains in ignored `.work`.

**Causal interpretation:** Source-document recall and merely narrowing a window around a known exact gold substring do not explain the observed disagreement. The next decisive check is **full propositional sufficiency** (subject, predicate, direction, date, negation, conditions, requested answer slot), using _independently source-reviewed_ annotations, plus correct Writer completion on the exact original authorized SOURCE. Do not convert a literal Reader extraction into permission to answer. No quality gate passed.

## 72. S2 — new controlled SOURCE→Reader→Writer relation/answer-role falsification (2026-10-27; synthetic DEV, no rollout)

**Causal question:** Can we reproduce the source-level temporal/directional answer error and distinguish it from source selection, quote extraction and Writer behavior **outside the exposed historical 200-question families**? Constructed and froze **four entirely fictional SOURCE documents** about a ceramics lab, an orchard shipment, a theater lighting plan and a water-station sample; they do not contain private source data. Each original is immutable and SHA-256 pinned for its own run. A new EN/ES set of **24 questions (12 intended answerable, 12 not explicitly answerable)** tests requested numbers, actors, inverse sender/recipient roles, procedural preconditions, temporal events and unknown pages/locations. The authored expected answer values were frozen _before_ model capture and withheld from model input. This is **author-defined synthetic truth, NOT independent human-reviewed gold**, and no actual index/ranking, reviewed KNOWLEDGE publication, authenticated HTTP Search, ContextPacket or access control was executed.

**Direct pipeline:** Give the full correct original SOURCE to the unchanged `qwen2.5:7b-instruct` Reader v5, check its source-literal quote/answer shape, then (only after literal Reader extraction) pass that **quoted passage alone** to the historical Writer v2 system prompt, Qwen model, temperature 0, seed 1317, three-field `ANSWER/ABSTAIN` schema and unchanged `SRC1` identifier. No second Qwen veto, QA answer hint or experimental Writer slots prompt. All **24/24** captures valid and complete. Observed **12/12** source-literal Reader proposals on author-labeled positives, **one** source-literal Reader support among 12 author-labeled negatives; Writer returned the expected regex-anchored value in **11/12** positives and **one** answer on the 12 negative controls. These structural counts are **not atomic-claim entailment accuracy** and should not be used as a precision estimate.

**Two source-exact cases explain the remaining ambiguity:**

- **Temporal actor/action conflation is real (water-station negative C24).** The SOURCE explicitly records **Sol verified on September 12, 2026** and **Vera transported on September 13, 2026**. Asked in Spanish **who verified the sample on September 13**, Reader v5 said `ANSWERS` citing the sentence about Vera's _transport on the 13th, after Sol verified it_; Writer answered **Sol**, but the source does **not** attest verification on September 13. It is a **fully source-literal, answer-shaped, but unsupported relation/time binding**, crossing both Reader and Writer boundaries. A citation hash and positive Reader verdict are inadequate semantic proofs.
- **Gold label/source mismatch invalidates a seeming Writer error (ceramics C01).** A question asked for the page of the **kiln** inspection report, but SOURCE only called it the **ceramics laboratory** inspection report, listed on page 42. The Reader extracted `42`; the Writer abstained. Because the extra `kiln` qualifier is not explicitly attached to that report, author-designating this as unquestionably positive was too strong. Its abstention cannot honestly be counted as a proven false negative without human review. This **also** demonstrates why even development gold labels must be checked against complete SOURCE roles and qualifiers.

**Paired quote-expansion falsification on 13 literal-Reader-positive questions:** Freeze the **identical** 12 author-positive + one reader-supported negative case; compare historical Writer baseline with `SHORTEST_READER_QUOTE` vs the **exact containing whole SOURCE sentence**, using the same question/model/prompt/seed. **26/26** valid outputs. There was **no recovered expected value**: both arms returned **11 of the twelve author-positive expected regex values**, abstained on disputed ceramics C01, and both answered unsafely on temporal C24. Source-sentence expansion alone did not correct the actor/date conflation nor the ambiguous gold label. No general rule to expand all citations follows.

**A distinct 14-question authored contrastive test** against the _same two original SOURCE documents_ held subject/date/action fixed while varying the requested actor, event date, direction and explicit negative. **Eight intended answerables/six unanswerables**, with unchanged Reader→Writer pipeline, all model calls valid. Reader proposed source-literal evidence for **8/8** intended answerables and **1/6** unanswerables; the latter inverted **who sent crates to whom**, but the Writer **abstained** rather than assert an unsupported recipient-to-sender role. Another negative reader ANSWERS had a **nonliteral** quote, correctly held before Writer. On eight positives, the Writer emitted seven semantically plausible correct answers, including the explicitly negative `Vera did not verify the sample`; one destination question abstained. The mechanical exact-answer regex scorer reports only **6/8** because it incorrectly expected a bare English `No` and rejected the fully explicit denial `Vera did not verify the sample`. Treat the 7/8 qualitative classification as **author inspection, not independent adjudication**. The otherwise similar `Who verified on September 13?` question was rejected in the new _English_ contrastive form, whereas the earlier _Spanish_ C24 was admitted. This is **input formulation/language sensitivity**, not a universal time-detection failure or a proven stable fix.

**Conclusions:** (1) SOURCE hashes, literal quote spans and authentic citations remain **necessary but insufficient**. (2) The causal semantic defect lies at **question↔complete proposition/role+time proof**, where Qwen occasionally changes a transport event into a verification event, plus the Writer's inconsistent ability to stop unsupported answers. (3) Passing only the shortest quote vs the complete original sentence did not repair the observed error. (4) Even self-authored gold can misclassify a partly matched relation as fully supported: independent source/claim labels and controlled source-family-independent EN/ES judging are still indispensable. This is a **narrow reproducible failure mode, not a repaired global S2 pipeline**.

**Private SHA-256:** synthetic 24-case frozen inputs `ebabc31b3bafbefcc278e771b97673e4820adc09e707606f76a49b1e7b43c0dc`; 24 model raw rows `bf557b6893de53d751264fefa346bfd26eed817a7213f820ea2bc1d492faaf75`; first stage score `101ed20cb191f73fac453d028bf61cf8172e37928ac8b800db71e18b81ecc6b6`. Sentence comparison manifest `111361b137e2ae3d5d399adf6ef74a23b933e60ef1a863c41f543e75c982d83b`; 26 raw outcomes `882e9142f0cd6f86db3496b5e7151a66e370527f4886c49d374ba910d6c76f06`; paired score `0145064a0852f4a0f4a81b4e6c595f7e8a37eedb1ff3d3fd0f7ec2ae4b9a80db`. Contrastive 14-case freeze `d537326825e8d3a0cc0b49be7ab9c99b9b088dcd56e84bcd4fee958921cb6833`; 14 raw results `82f44eb8d6983f9009ee102297504daa0364164a8b70fe4b589cfc95e7d0ec8d`; scorer `245d140d88490ed02ce1cdc10aebeeca91e2b68eddd0ac16da1802457f105140`. Everything remains private in ignored `.work`.

**Next gateable test and safe implementation boundary:** Do **not** enable a new LLM judge, a global keyword/date heuristic, indiscriminate read-window expansion or the failed §70 Writer prompt. The genuine next acceptance unit is a human-reviewed **full natural-language question → atomic affirmative/negative claim, with exact subject/actor, predicate, direction, temporal event, modality/condition and answer slot → original authorized SOURCE passage → all Writer statements and citations**. Run that on new source/publisher/relation-family-disjoint EN/ES samples and report paired true-positive retention **and** false support/false abstention with source-family clustering and actual complete HTTP p95. The owner's 127 X/Y packets remain unreviewed and unchanged. **S2 OPEN, E5 SHADOW, LAYERED OFF, retry OFF, S3–S5 BLOCKED, PR #38 DRAFT/UNMERGED.** These experiments produced **no production code or configuration change**; documentation only. Historical §67–§70 local commits were not remotely published owing to a blocked GitHub tree action; no corresponding remote CI or merge is claimed.

## 73. S2 — existing production LAYERED composite measured with real Qwen Reader→guard→Writer, with reversed-arm p95 check (2026-10-27; DEV only)

**Hypothesis and architecture:** The owner asked whether enabling the already implemented optional mechanisms together (E5, LAYERED, typed source answer, structural guard, confirmation/retry and Writer) might meet semantic sufficiency. This experiment did **not** turn on default production functionality or instantiate an alternative admission engine. It exercised the actual `ReaderEvidenceVerifier` (unchanged Qwen2.5 v5 prompt and typed source-value binding), `QueryConditionedSemanticEvidenceReader`, `LayeredEvidenceAdmissionPipeline`, `assessRetrievalAnswerabilityWithLayeredAdmission` and its structural guard, passing only an admitted source-quote span to the **unchanged historical baseline Writer**. This is the exact domain-layer composition available behind the current optional HTTP LAYERED mode, not a direct-model Reader imitation.

**What is and is not in this run:** Input contains exactly one original full-document `SearchHit` per question from the **four SHA-pinned, newly authored fictional SOURCE families** of §72. It preserves revision/source bytes and cites literal admitted excerpt spans but has no actual PostgreSQL, E5 embedding search, authenticated Fastify, real approved Git KNOWLEDGE, cross-vault permissions, ContextPacket or external source-custody verification. Docker Desktop was offline (`dockerDesktopLinuxEngine` unavailable), so a claimed full HTTP/E5 end-to-end p95 would be fabricated. The component-accurate **domain pipeline** and its per-question actual Qwen calls **were** executed; 24 author-designed EN/ES questions (12 provisionally answerable, 12 negative), two configurations, 48 captures per order, and two complete runs. The author's expected values/negatives are **developer synthetic labels** and include one disputed qualifier noted in §72, **not independently reviewed ground truth**.

**One-factor arms:** `CURRENT_V5_LAYERED`: existing v5 reader, one-candidate (single source), no `confirmQuoteSufficiency` and no `retryUnboundValueOnce`. `CONFIRM_AND_RETRY_LAYERED`: same reader, source, guard, source-bound value, Qwen and Writer but enables _both existing_ optional quote confirmation (second source-only Qwen read) and bounded unbound-value retry. Both use actual `OpenAICompatibleEvidenceReader` with structured schema, actual `LayeredEvidenceAdmissionPipeline`, 25,000ms batch limit/22,000ms provider limit, same questions/quotes, no post-hoc answer hints, and Writer only when a LAYERED candidate has a visible source-bound `SUPPORTS` span. Every admitted source excerpt was the original synthetic SOURCE text. The arm order was **reversed in a second run**; two frozen `FREEZE.json` manifests were **byte-identical** and SHA-256 verified before either readout. Local Ollama required fresh startup in the first run, which is reported as operational failure rather than semantic wrong answer.

| Metric (24 cases per arm)                                                        | First sequence: LAYERED then confirm+retry | Reversed sequence: confirm+retry then LAYERED |
| -------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------ | --------------------------------------------- |
| Positive evidence admitted (author-labeled 12)                                   | baseline **11/12**, extra **9/12**         | baseline **12/12**, extra **10/12**           |
| Positive answer with the frozen expected literal value                           | baseline **10/12**, extra **9/12**         | baseline **10/12**, extra **9/12**            |
| Negative application candidate admitted (author-labeled 12)                      | **1/12** in both                           | **1/12** in both                              |
| Negative Writer ANSWER emission                                                  | **1/12** in both                           | **1/12** in both                              |
| Extra variant positive answers lost relative to paired baseline                  | **1**, recovered **0**                     | **1**, recovered **0**                        |
| Per-case observed **domain** pipeline p95 (Reader→LAYERED→Writer where admitted) | baseline **19,546 ms**, extra **7,259 ms** | baseline **3,662 ms**, extra **5,447 ms**     |
| Admission-only per-case p95                                                      | baseline **18,332 ms**, extra **6,011 ms** | baseline **2,413 ms**, extra **4,348 ms**     |

**Interpretation:** The first sequence's latency comparison is **confounded by cold model startup/order**: the first prompt in each arm produced `READER_ERROR:EVIDENCE_READER_TIMEOUT` (one per arm), but the script's `errors=0` field counts uncaught provider/schema failures and does not count these fail-closed Reader decisions. Reversing the arm order with the same original and request settings yielded warm **3.662s vs 5.447s**, about **49% higher p95** for additional confirmation/retry, without blocking the one developer-defined negative Writer emission and with **one less expected positive answer** in both runs. These small, correlated four-source-family p95 values are **not production p95** and prove neither steady-state SLO nor operational cost/benefit on a real multi-unit retrieval pool. The repeated output loss is a falsification of blanket optional-feature promotion on the present evidence, not a universal proof against confirmation on some future cohort.

**Architecture: what is already integrated versus really disconnected:** The live Fastify `server.ts` already builds `ReaderEvidenceVerifier` → `QueryConditionedSemanticEvidenceReader` → `LayeredEvidenceAdmissionPipeline` when explicitly configured for LAYERED. ADR 0006 already propagates typed source-bound answer value into the guard and validates source slices, and `s2-admission-shadow` preserves actual admission traces without leaking raw text. Evaluation packages already include original-quote replay, frozen reader choice, question/answer claim projection, semantic F3, per-citation reconciliation, answer-completeness and an E2E diagnostic trace. **But** `assessRetrievalAnswerabilityWithLayeredAdmission` constructs HTTP inputs with only `{ query, hit }`, not independently verified `queryProposition` / `candidateProposition`. Thus the `ExactStructuredPropositionMatcher` is **not currently making normal HTTP LAYERED relations complete**. Supplying guessed propositions or enabling a second same-model judge would violate the source-first design and past DEV veto evidence; extra confirmations do **not** provide independent entailment of actor, predicate, direction, precise event date, negation, conditions, quantified value or final Writer statement-level claims. The §72 Spanish verification-date false support persisted in the configured domain composition. E5 is already the indexed candidate-retrieval layer when configured, **not** a semantic entailment engine.

**Preregistration and gates:** `docs/architecture/s2-postcalibration-preregistration.md` requires source/publisher-/relation-family-disjoint original EN/ES cases, authentic human dual review/third adjudication, source-bounded question+claim support and contradiction, per-claim Writer citation proof, actual authorized HTTP full-request cold/warm p95 <=25s, FAR <=0.10 and no answer regressions. This synthetic lab cannot satisfy those gates. The independent 127 X/Y packets are unchanged and remain for owner review. **NO DEFAULT CHANGE, NO LAYERED/confirm/retry PROMOTION, NO PR MERGE**; E5 stays SHADOW, S2 OPEN and S3–S5 BLOCKED.

**Reproducible ignored-private artifacts:** Both sets of 24 original questions/four source hashes and two configurations were frozen to the same `FREEZE.json` SHA `088c1296e2d3f8c52146426101f81b23f2cc5422f37d526d42455da1e9b6ee12`. First 48 raw model outcomes SHA `ed17894f7b43603e46859cda3865bc635c2841626006814ef3c1f6ff12de1b7d`; first summary SHA `6b32dc24c2d2d24e2f3f179007826e35a889a5a5eeeb775f481f0295fb26df0f`. Reversed-order 48 raw SHA `0483a73630b1bd734ef8c65780f5d6acc9dbd5a02c8b4440af109ec0b577d334`; reversed summary SHA `7acfc5c0c6963ccda0ead9aac3f2f5ca785156ca480e00a7d8d13e3dbe759233`. A pair of executable private TypeScript scripts and original results remain inside ignored `.work`; no source questions, model text, secrets or test gold are committed.

**Next controlled integration:** Get real authorized SOURCE and reviewed KNOWLEDGE through PostgreSQL/E5 into HTTP/LAYERED (Docker and credentials permitting); freeze raw per-candidate unit hashes, typed requested answer, Reader quote, original source offsets, token use and claim statement before adjudication. Bind **independently verified question and original-source propositions** (not made-up ones) at the existing **single** `LayeredEvidenceAdmissionPipeline` owner boundary, then use existing per-claim reconciliation and whole-answer audit on the unchanged Writer output. Compare paired positive retention, false support and unnecessary abstention against baseline, with true end-to-end request p95 and release gates. Do not create multiple incompatible permanent verifiers for the same requirement.

## 74. S2 — retire historical X/Y as prerequisite; exact qualified-proposition comparison in one existing admission owner (2026-10-27; no semantic gate)

**Historical reviewer packet disposition:** The 127 X/Y packets generated in §62 on October 26 are a blinded _historical source-supplied Reader v5_ sample of the 261-question frozen capture. They are not paired current full E5→authorized LAYERED→ContextPacket→Writer cases, do not contain final Writer answer assertions, do not verify current HTTP authorization/freshness, and did not use new independently adjudicated source families. Their private receipt was reaudited today and returned `PACKETS_VERIFIED_UNREVIEWED`, `caseCount=127`, `independentHumanReviewVerified=false`. Original X SHA-256 `a66b50e384aa2f7ceb0d4bcbece4c01e2ff50029dc6c7167a9268d9cc0a3ae3a` and Y SHA-256 `b89d7017d4dbd83f143498e797626d2e496794a25cc0f837d8254c9f3f8b4f37` are unchanged. The owner need **not** review these historical packets to unblock development; leave their private inputs/submission skeletons intact as non-independent DEV diagnostics. This does **not** waive independent human review to _accept_ S2. As formalized in `s2-postcalibration-preregistration.md`, freeze a new source-family-disjoint EN/ES set before any model outcome is inspected, then review current end-to-end admitted/withheld quotes, entire Writer answers and atomic-claim support with two independent reviewers and third adjudication. Do not sample only the successful outputs and do not reuse the already exposed historical X/Y labels.

**Confirmed product-contract defect:** The sole existing `ExactStructuredPropositionMatcher` compared subject, predicate, optional object and polarity. Additional query/candidate qualifiers (temporal event, conditions, modality, units) were ignored when a caller supplied them, potentially yielding `ANSWERS` for the **wrong time** with identical actor/predicate/object. The existing governed shadow rejects such unsupported qualifier inputs, but the shared proposition interface lacked typed support: ordinary `LAYERED` still passes no question/source projections through its HTTP `{query,hit}` batch inputs (§48, §73). These are two different problems and cannot be conflated.

**One-owner bounded correction:** Extended `StructuredPropositionQuery` and `StructuredProposition` inside `packages/retrieval/src/evidence-admission.ts` with **optional explicit** `temporalScope`, `conditions`, `modality`, `quantityUnit` fields. The _existing_ `ExactStructuredPropositionMatcher` now compares both sides for exact normalized qualifier agreement; absent/empty/changed temporal and unit values, changed modality, and missing/altered conditions return `RELATED_NOT_ANSWERING` rather than `ANSWERS`. Condition order is irrelevant only for equivalent complete sets (trim/case/spacing); duplicate/empty entries cannot authenticate a match. When a query omits qualifiers but a source proposition is explicitly qualified, the matcher withholds automatic unconditional support. Already supported unqualified exact pairs keep their previous behavior. No heuristic extracts events, translations, source-specific terminology, guessed dates or equivalence from free text, and no model receives admission authority by agreement. `apps/api/src/governed-proposition-shadow.ts` now reuses the **same** exported `StructuredPropositionQuery` type rather than redeclaring qualifiers; it **still rejects** unsupported qualifiers on its shadow path until authorized, independently established original-question projection and SOURCE event semantics exist. It does not treat temporal _fact validity_ as source _event time_.

**Red-before-green regression:** Two new negative tests first failed on the previous implementation: exact same subject/predicate/object but wrong event date returned `ANSWERS`, and a governed pipeline returned that unsafe result without invoking its semantic reader. After the change, the tests enforce mismatch rejection and no semantic fallback, including the general fallback path when a known explicit temporal conflict exists. Separate tests cover mismatched/missing source time, preconditions, modality and quantity unit; matching qualifiers still yield `ANSWERS`. Focused `evidence-admission.test.ts` **17/17 PASS**; full `@akp/retrieval` **699/699 PASS**, `@akp/evaluation` **448/448 PASS**, and standard `@akp/api` **150/150 PASS on final sequential rerun**; `@akp/retrieval` and `@akp/api` typecheck PASS. The API suite initially recorded **149/150** because `health.test.ts` exceeded its existing 30-second timeout; an isolated diagnostic retry with a longer **test-runner-only** timeout passed in 19.6s, and the unchanged standard suite then passed all 150 including health in 9.9s. Neither the production timeout nor test source was changed. Keep the initial flaky timing documented, not hidden as an uninterrupted all-pass. Tests prove a generic necessary exactness rule on explicitly supplied projections, **not** the actual natural-language semantic sufficiency of this user's complete S2 runtime.

**Explicit remaining gap:** The normal HTTP route supplies `{query,hit}`, not independently certified query and SOURCE proposition fields. A source-hash-valid date and a model-generated slot are neither independent proof of question role nor sufficient to answer. The future authorized projector and ContextPacket/Writer per-claim verifier must keep source event time distinct from temporal-fact validity, and require every requested actor, predicate, direction, polarity, condition, quantity unit and answer role. Do not promote LAYERED/default retry, claim better live recall/FAR or certify p95 from these tests. S2 OPEN, E5 SHADOW, LAYERED OFF by default, S3–S5 BLOCKED, PR #38 DRAFT/UNMERGED.

## 75. S2 — Candidate-identity collision cannot borrow source support across revisions or the verifier window (2026-10-27)

**Question:** If retrieval passes two `SearchHit` objects carrying the same public `documentId:unitId` key, can a positive Reader verdict for one SOURCE excerpt be assigned to the other excerpt or a hit **outside the bounded reading window**? The public `retrievalAnswerabilityCandidateKey` intentionally omits vault/revision/excerpt. In `assessRetrievalAnswerabilityWithLayeredAdmission`, `traces` was indexed by that key and reused for every matching candidate signal; the first verified excerpt could therefore promote two different revisions, including an out-of-window one. The original optional `assessRetrievalAnswerabilityWithVerifier` also stored verifier decisions in a `Map` keyed only by document/unit, and a later passage could overwrite a prior result. This is a concrete **identity/provenance alias**, distinct from the previous lack of actor/date entailment.

**Red-before-green evidence:** Adversarial tests supply an answer-bearing unit ("3 attempts") and a conflicting stale unit ("9 attempts") with identical document and unit IDs but different `revision` and `excerpt`. Before the fix, the LAYERED assessment incorrectly returned `supported: true` even when the stale duplicate was beyond `maxCandidates:1`. With a third unrelated unit, it duplicated the source-bound support key; both tests were **red on the pre-change implementation**. A distinct optional `ENFORCE` verifier regression was likewise red before the shared fix. A fourth regression showed the **deterministic baseline** also claimed `supported: true` on a duplicate source identity before the correction. The tests include a noncolliding control so blanket abstention cannot pass spuriously.

**Single-owner repair:** `packages/retrieval/src/answerability.ts` now owns `collidingRetrievalCandidateKeys`, one shared O(n) pre-read identity scan. **The deterministic baseline itself also fails closed on ambiguous keys** with `CANDIDATE_KEY_COLLISION`, retaining unrelated evidence signals and comparison/vector diagnostics; this prevents raw default support from exporting an ambiguous candidate ID. Both `LAYERED` and optional query-conditioned `SHADOW/ENFORCE` readers consume the same collision set **across all input hits, not just those inside the read window**. For every ambiguous key, all associated verified traces are `INSUFFICIENT`, with respective audit reasons `LAYERED_CANDIDATE_KEY_COLLISION` or `QUERY_CONDITIONED_CANDIDATE_KEY_COLLISION`; none is sent to a Reader/verification model. All unique in-window hits still enter the existing pipeline unchanged and preserve their order. `ENFORCE` also revokes a colliding `DIRECT_CHANNEL_SUPPORT` rather than letting a deterministic shortcut reauthorize an ambiguous identity. `SHADOW` remains observational and retains the **now collision-safe** deterministic baseline's supported/abstained decision; it does not promote a shadow verifier verdict. An entirely collided verifier window invokes **zero** provider calls, including zero empty `verifyBatch` calls.

**Scope and acceptance:** No public key format, HTTP contract, database migration, source file, model prompt, E5, Writer, default flags, previously exposed DEV oracle or reviewer packet changed. Rejecting duplicates can intentionally increase abstentions when upstream retrieval contains duplicate units; this is safer than falsely asserting a source and should prompt upstream deduplication **with revision/provenance checks**, not silently passing the first/last copy. A valid, unique original quote still does **not** prove the full question proposition or Writer claim. No complete authenticated SOURCE→reviewed KNOWLEDGE→E5→LAYERED→ContextPacket→Writer request or end-to-end p95 was measured by this deterministic regression. Historical X/Y remain `PACKETS_VERIFIED_UNREVIEWED`, not a new release-gate cohort.

**Validation:** two initial LAYERED, one optional ENFORCE and one deterministic-base adversarial test failed against their respective pre-change implementations; they now pass, along with the out-of-window `ENFORCE/SHADOW` no-model-call control. Focused `layered-answerability.test.ts` **10/10 PASS**, `answerability.test.ts` **139/139 PASS**; `@akp/retrieval` full **704/704 PASS**, Retrieval typecheck PASS. `@akp/evaluation` initial parallel run **447/448** with unrelated `ann-decision.test.ts` exceeding its original 5s test timeout; that file then **2/2 PASS** isolated and full unchanged suite **448/448 PASS** sequentially, without modifying its test/timeout. API typecheck PASS; the API suite intermittently failed only `health.test.ts` on cold/loaded runs (**149/150**, twice) but the test passed **1/1** in isolation at the original 30s threshold and the final **unchanged standard API suite passed 150/150** on a warm rerun (health 10.5s). Do not erase that startup instability or claim a consistent cold-start pass. Formatting, documents, contracts, generality, hygiene, secrets and structural-only holdout validations passed locally; rerun after the final note change. Strict S2 independent source/claim truth and actual HTTP p95 remain **not verified**.

**Next semantic integration:** Run authorized original-question→atomic-claim grounding with SOURCE-governed full proposition and event-time (not mere temporal fact validity) at the already existing R5 admission owner. Rebind each selected quote to immutable original SOURCE under the current authorization/revision, then verify each Writer statement's requested actor, predicate, object/direction, negation, event time, conditions, modality and exact answer role. Compare fresh source-family-disjoint EN/ES positives and hard negatives on identical full HTTP pipelines with cold/warm p95; request new blinded human review only for this prospective frozen architecture, not the historical X/Y packets. **S2 OPEN; E5 SHADOW; LAYERED OFF by default; retry OFF; S3–S5 BLOCKED; PR #38 DRAFT/UNMERGED.**

## 76. S2 — Governed complete-question/SOURCE proposition bound into existing R5 and Writer-claim custody (2026-10-27; opt-in, NOT S2 acceptance)

**Problem and architecture:** §74 made the existing `ExactStructuredPropositionMatcher` aware of actor/predicate/object/polarity/event time/conditions/modality/units; §75 protected per-unit revision identity. However, normal `assessRetrievalAnswerabilityWithLayeredAdmission` still sent only `{query,hit}` to the actual R5 `LayeredEvidenceAdmissionPipeline`; its structured relation proof was disconnected. Subsequent Writer full-answer checks existed as evaluation diagnostics but did not consume an original-question+source-bound structured claim proof. No NLP model, vector index or hash comparison can make its own reading semantically authoritative; this increment instead adds a **narrow internal attestation contract** at the existing R5 boundary, with a strict provenance check and opt-in downstream statement audit. It does not solve the remaining independent semantic adjudication.

**Opt-in question/SOURCE port (Retrieval):** Added `GovernedLayeredPropositionPort` and `GovernedLayeredPropositionProof` in `packages/retrieval/src/layered-answerability.ts`. An internal source service must return the full **original question and SHA-256**, exact candidate key/document/unit/vault/revision/path, raw original SOURCE `Uint8Array` bytes plus SHA-256 and strict UTF-8 decoded text, exact excerpt UTF-16 boundaries inside those original bytes, independently established `queryProposition`, and a source-grounded `candidateProposition` whose exact quote lies inside that same excerpt. The binder checks every identity and hash/byte/span relationship; malformed, absent, changed, exception-throwing or mismatched proof yields `GOVERNED_SOURCE_PROPOSITION_UNAVAILABLE` and **never invokes the semantic Reader**. Its structural hash checks do **not** authenticate the service or prove original narrative entailment; the application service must revalidate current authorization, original revision, text custody and meaning separately. When the port is not configured, the existing LAYERED behavior is unchanged.

**Exactly one admission owner:** The existing `LayeredEvidenceAdmissionPipeline` gained the internal-only `requireStructuredProposition` input flag. In this mode missing projections and structured subject/predicate mismatches **fail closed**, even when the legacy pipeline constructor selects `SEMANTIC_FALLBACK`. Exact governed supported quote still runs through the current `StructuralEvidenceGuard`; known direction, actor, polarity, event-date, condition and object conflicts cannot be promoted by another model read. Candidate identity collisions from §75 are still rejected **before** resolving original SOURCE. No public HTTP or MCP payload permits the client to set this flag, provide a proposition or pass in original bytes.

**Request-scoped API wiring, OFF unless explicitly injected:** `apps/api/src/server.ts` and `apps/api/src/routes/search.ts` now permit internal `ApiServerDependencies.governedPropositionPort` (optional). In both authorized Search and ContextPack retrieval use cases, `scopeGovernedLayeredPropositionPort` checks the **current principal's resolved vault+path authorization** _before_ invoking original SOURCE lookup, and forwards `principalId,spaceId` to that service; the proof is then rebound by Retrieval to the exact hit revision and bytes. The resolver is **not** constructed by default, and the repository does not yet have an authorized PostgreSQL-backed original-source semantic certification implementation for arbitrary free-text propositions. Passing a test-injected fake port does not establish real user identity or semantic correctness. E5 and LAYERED default configuration is unchanged.

**Writer-claim custody reuses R5, not a second truth engine:** Added `packages/retrieval/src/governed-writer-claims.ts` (`auditGovernedWriterStatements`). The caller supplies the ACTUAL Writer answer and an explicit list of exact UTF-16-covered statements with citation candidate keys and **separately verified statement propositions**. Every character in the answer must be covered by the ordered statement spans, every citation key must match the exact selected hit, and each hit is rebound to original authenticated-source service output. For _each_ statement, the **same** `LayeredEvidenceAdmissionPipeline` first tests the full original question projection against the cited original SOURCE proposition, then tests the actual Writer statement projection against that same SOURCE proposition. Any missing/out-of-range span, wrong source/citation, unsupported original-question actor/time/condition/direction, fabricated Writer relation or extra unsupported second sentence withholds the candidate. It never calls the semantic model to override known conflicts. The result is deliberately `SOURCE_BOUND_CLAIMS_CANDIDATE` **not an assertion of verified atomicity, independent full-question coverage or production release**; question clause coverage and statement splitting still require independent review.

**Existing E2E diagnostic reused, including the Writer's actual citation identity:** `packages/evaluation/src/s2-end-to-end-evidence-trace.ts` now accepts this optional Writer-statement audit result. Every claimed statement carries a native Writer `citationId` and the SHA-256 of the original raw SOURCE bytes checked by the governed port. The end-to-end trace refuses a positive Writer-claim packet unless **every** audited citation ID belongs to the actual frozen Writer `citations` list **and** the exact `sourcePassages` snapshot sent to the provider contains the same citation ID with the same original file SHA-256; every Writer citation must have at least one covered claim. This stops an unrelated attested source from laundering a positive claim against a different source passed into the Writer. A negative/malformed or incomplete result yields `GOVERNED_WRITER_CLAIMS_WITHHELD` at `CLAIM`; a positive candidate continues through the **existing F3, citation and complete-answer** diagnostics without production authority. Without this optional result, existing traces remain backward compatible. Tests mutate the frozen original SHA and Writer source ID independently to prove rejection. This is the opt-in evaluation handoff, NOT a new live Writer endpoint or an automatic audit of unprojected model statements.

**Adversarial developer tests:** Added two original-SOURCE R5 tests for positive support and rejected subject, predicate, object/direction, polarity, date, condition; for original-question SHA, candidate ID, vault, path, revision, original-byte SHA, mismatched/invalid UTF-8 bytes, raw-text mutation, excerpt offsets, quote bounds, and failing resolver. Added ten per-Writer-statement tests for a one-claim candidate and fail-closed wrong actor, direction, predicate, negation, temporal event, missing preconditions, extra unsupported second sentence, citation mismatch, uncovered answer bytes, altered SOURCE revision and wrong original-question role. Added API test of principal/space forwarding, denied path/vault before storage-port invocation, and absent-port behavior, plus an end-to-end trace test proving that observed claim custody mismatch blocks the candidate while a positive remains shadow-only. These tests use author-constructed text and governed fixture projections: **they cannot measure real semantic recall, false-admission rate, independently correct gold or actual full-request p95**.

**Critical not-yet-complete work:** (1) Build/validate an actual **authenticated** PostgreSQL/Git original-SOURCE-backed service port, not a test mock, with per-principal source artifact/verified-revision custody; (2) establish trustworthy full original-question and _per-Writer-statement_ proposition projections from EN/ES text, including requested answer role, event vs fact-validity time, actor/direction, negation and conjunctions, without promoting a model's self-rating; (3) connect the active Writer adapter to real HTTP ContextPacket citations and source rebind; (4) measure equal-cohort, source-family-disjoint EN/ES positive retention and wrong-admission/abstention, with actual authenticated full HTTP cold/warm p95, before the new pre-frozen independent human adjudication. The historical 127 X/Y packets remain unchanged and `PACKETS_VERIFIED_UNREVIEWED`. **S2 OPEN, E5 SHADOW, LAYERED OFF by default, no changed reader retries, S3–S5 BLOCKED, PR #38 DRAFT/UNMERGED.**

## 77. S2 — Is the vault or absence of a paid API key preventing ordinary hybrid/vector RAG? Live isolated PostgreSQL + real local E5 diagnostic (2026-10-27)

**Question and distinction:** Do not confuse `vault` (space/path permission, original-source/revision-scoped ownership, one active embedding generation per vault) with a special semantic matching algorithm. Standard document RAG indexes selected text units, creates embeddings and performs top-k vector or hybrid keyword+vector retrieval. AKP already has `knowledge_units`, `unit_embeddings`, `embedding_generations`, dimension-specific pgvector/HNSW indexes, lexical GIN fields, explicit vector/lexical/exact channels, rank fusion, optional graph, and query-conditioned R5 admission. This is an ordinary PostgreSQL/pgvector hybrid retrieval substrate with additional provenance and authorization. Neither a vector rank nor a source quote SHA establishes **that a whole question/Writer proposition is entailed**. Treat vault membership as a _necessary scope boundary_, not as an independently proven quality bottleneck. The original 200-question DEV audit (§200 earlier sections/README in private `.work/s2-pipeline-stage200-20261027`) already found the reference document in the Reader's 64 candidates for 126/126 provisionally answerable questions; 49 lacked any historically adjudicated sufficient citation, including 22 where the reference document was not admitted and 27 where it was admitted without sufficient quote. Another 31 abstained despite at least one historically deemed sufficient quote. Those DEV citation judgments are NOT independent gold; exploratory HTTP top10 is NOT the admitted/reader-64 superset. Vault or vector absence cannot explain away downstream source-unit/Reader/Writer losses for those 126 questions.

**Configuration audit, no credentials exposed:** In the existing development-root `.env`, `AKP_VECTOR_ENABLED=true` and `AKP_EMBEDDING_PROVIDER=local-multilingual-e5`. No external embedding API key or hosted reader API/model configured in that file; local Ollama had Qwen2.5, Qwen3.5, Gemma3 and Llama3.2 model names available, and the CPU E5 model cache existed. These are _configuration/installed-model observations_, **not a claim that a production service had an active, populated pgvector generation or real HTTP E5 runs for all 200 questions**. The normal Reader can use an OpenAI-compatible URL/model/API-key pair, but a key merely authenticates a selected hosted service; it does not supply original SOURCE semantic certification, principal authorization, complete Writer claim support, independent review or a guaranteed accuracy/p95 improvement. Do not insert secrets into issue comments or reports; an external LLM A/B needs explicit residency/privacy approval and matched cost/latency/quality measurements.

**Genuine isolated integration (not mock-only tests):** Used a newly created disposable **pgvector/pgvector:pg16** container bound to loopback, with a strictly disposable test database name accepted by `assertTestDatabaseSafety`. Applied all **92 unmodified DB migrations** to it. The first trial with an ambiguous test database name triggered the expected built-in `TEST_DATABASE_NOT_DISPOSABLE` guard (11 blocked tests); the test was **re-run against a differently named database in the same disposable container**, with no database safety flag/bypass changed and no user/workspace database touched.

**Observed green results:**

- `apps/api/test/lexical-ranking.integration.test.ts`: **8/8 PASS** against real PostgreSQL; includes acronym/lexical selection, structural unit rows, Spanish accent handling and wrong-relation abstention.
- `apps/api/test/semantic-retrieval.integration.test.ts`: **2/2 PASS** against real PostgreSQL; covers cross-vault/space and path scope, active generation, lexical fallback on unavailable vector provider, revision-aware vector eligibility and repair.
- `apps/api/test/projection-vector-preservation.integration.test.ts`: **1/1 PASS**; preserves existing active embedding generation on provider failure and switches only on successful rebuilding.
- `apps/api/test/real-semantic-retrieval.integration.test.ts`: **1/1 PASS** with the actual cached **intfloat/multilingual-e5-small** `384d` model (`AKP_RUN_REAL_SEMANTIC_TEST=1`, `AKP_LOCAL_FILES_ONLY=1`); built and queried real embeddings in pgvector, ranked relevant and distractor passages in EN/ES, tested federated/nonfederated vault scope and negative-answer candidate behavior. The test's ~15.4s test body / ~26.4s runner includes setup, indexing and requests; **NOT the application full HTTP p95**. A successful synthetic cross-language retrieval test does not measure recall on the historical 200-question corpus.
- All these integration tests used **synthetic fixture documents seeded into an isolated disposable DB**. No credentials, private SOURCE text, manual X/Y responses or new acceptance gold were committed.

**Architectural conclusion:** There is currently NO evidence that the vault abstraction, pgvector schema or absence of a paid embedding key intrinsically causes the 49/126 early-source-sufficiency losses and 31 downstream Writer abstentions observed in historical DEV. Actual PostgreSQL and E5 proved mechanically functional in one controlled fixture; this does NOT exclude ranking/chunking/shortlist/permission defects in a different real vault or corpus, nor does it establish S2 sufficiency. The correct next falsifiable experiment is an equal-case funnel **on real authorized HTTP** with per-question `vector/lexical/exact` channel membership, top-64 and actual Reader-window unit IDs, exact original SOURCE quote, Reader decision, admitted evidence, Writer claim/citations; compare a minimal **vector+lexical → same Reader/Writer** baseline against current LAYERED **without treating retrieval score as truth**, using same authorized scope, model, budget and cold/warm order. Report **retrieval@k, SOURCE-unit gold@k, Reader acceptance, false support and correct Writer answers** separately. The existing §73 extra confirmations/retry A/B did not improve its 24-case synthetic result, so don't simply activate every optional mechanism or promote a stronger cloud LLM without controlled data.

**Release boundaries unchanged:** §76 governed structured full-question/source and each Writer statement remains an _opt-in custody contract_, with no independently certified natural-language proposition extractor or real HTTP integrated Writer acceptance. E5 may be configured but LAYERED and retries are not promoted. Historical X/Y remains `PACKETS_VERIFIED_UNREVIEWED` and can be replaced by **new** independent packets after the full pipeline stabilizes. **S2 OPEN, S3–S5 BLOCKED, PR #38 DRAFT/UNMERGED; no claimed full 200-case live evaluation or end-to-end p95 in this diagnostic.**

**One direct tracing repair, not another gate:** Inspection of the _existing_ opt-in S2 application admission shadow (`apps/api/src/s2-admission-shadow.ts`) revealed that its redacted per-candidate diagnostics contained the hashed revision, candidate key, Reader result and excerpt/quote fingerprints but **did not record which actual first-stage channel retrieved each hit**. Added `retrievalChannelRanks` per candidate, with only an allowlist of `exact`, `lexical`, `vector`, `graph`, `graph-ppr`, `community` and bounded positive minimum rank per channel. Free-form contribution reasons, model/provider metadata, raw scores and all question/SOURCE text are excluded. Existing full-scope validation, hashed identifiers, admission decisions and shadow best-effort isolation are unchanged. A new adversarial test proves multiple duplicate vector ranks coalesce to the best rank, lexical is recorded independently, unknown/malformed channels and provider strings cannot escape, and application admission remains identical to the baseline (**6/6 focused S2 admission shadow tests PASS; API typecheck PASS**). **This does not retroactively recover missing per-channel provenance from the historical 200 requests**, does not ensure the active vector index was used, and is not a new semantic admission policy. It enables the next captured HTTP funnel to say concretely whether the right SOURCE arrived through vector, lexical or another channel _before_ measuring Reader/Writer losses.

## 78. S2 — Primary-source research and controlled BM25/metadata-classification boundary (2026-10-27)

**User hypothesis / architectural inventory:** Teams will not all follow one Obsidian vault folder convention, so document _meaning_ should be an independently evaluated virtual classification proposal rather than the physical path or vault identifier. ADR [0005 D3](../adr/0005-multilingual-retrieval-untrusted-sources-classification.md) and [roadmap FOLLOW_UP](core-redesign-roadmap.md) **already specify** the profile-scoped, multilingual, multi-label, uncertainty-aware non-destructive classifier with `UNKNOWN`, explicit source/revision hashes and review. No automatic whole-document classifier is currently authorized/published by that ADR; the physical vault remains an essential scope/permission and revision boundary even if the proposal recommends a different virtual category. Before enabling classification facets or hard filters, test whether they preserve the answer-bearing SOURCE unit and cross-vault ACL. This follows ADR D4: source/index integrity and S2 sufficiency evaluation precede durable classifier cutover and B5 migrations.

**Verified real implementation:** AKP currently does NOT implement BM25 under a different name. Its SQL first-stage lexical retrieval in `apps/api/src/routes/search.ts` uses `plainto_tsquery('simple', query)`, GIN-based `tsvector` matching and field-weighted `ts_rank_cd`, plus constrained assertion recall, while the vector path uses pgvector/HNSW (E5) and the existing channel fusion uses ranks. Its optional multilingual query transformer, section/atomic-unit candidates and R5/LAYERED Reader are _already present_ and must be reused in A/B arms.
